### [1] [Edge-cusped compactons from gradient-power nonlinear dispersion: exact transitions and singular spectral structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00552)
*Francisco R. Villatoro*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文引入一类含两个指数的守恒型色散方程族（非线性通量指数 p、梯度色散指数 r），可分离紧化、自由边界正则性与线性化谱结构三个机制，同时保留显式行波理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个同时包含几何奇异、弱可容许性、谱结构与数值正则性的统一退化色散模型，以便对紧子（compacton）的多重机制进行可解析的对照研究。

Method: 提出双指数守恒色散族（r=1 退化为绝对 Rosenau-Hyman 方程）；推导两条独立相变曲线 pr=1 与 r(p-1)=2；对 r≥1 将线性化约化为奇异 Sturm-Liouville 算子的 Friedrichs 实现；通过紧子适配变量替换化为 Jacobi-超几何型本征方程；并辅以 Fourier 尾部测量、投影误差与 Fourier-Galerkin 时间积分及 Jacobi-Galerkin 谱诊断。

Result: pr=1 区分无限尾孤立波与紧子；r(p-1)=2 区分具有正则自由边界的紧子与斜率发散的尖边紧子（后者仍为分布解，且自由边界与波峰处均无奇异测度）；振幅-速度律不变，但宽度除 r=1 外依赖振幅；谱上得到两个显式谱序列、恰一个负本征值与一维平移核；数值上 Fourier 尾部与投影误差符合自由边界指数预测的速率，尖波在 Fourier-Galerkin 积分中相干传播且相位漂移小，截断点谱未检出实部。

Conclusion: 该双指数族为在单一退化色散模型内统一研究紧子的几何奇异、弱可容许性、谱结构与数值正则性提供了显式测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce a conservative two-exponent dispersive family, with $p>1$ the nonlinear-flux exponent and $r>0$ the gradient-dispersion exponent, that separates the mechanisms governing compactification, free-boundary regularity and linearised spectral structure while retaining an explicit travelling-wave theory. The family reduces to the absolute Rosenau-Hyman equation when $r=1$. The deformation leaves the amplitude-speed law unchanged, but makes the width amplitude dependent except at $r=1$. Two independent transition curves emerge: $pr=1$ separates infinite-tail solitary waves from compactons, whereas $r(p-1)=2$ separates compactons with a regular free boundary from edge-cusped compactons with divergent slope. The latter remain distributional solutions, with no singular measures at either the free boundary or the crest. For $r\ge1$, the linearisation factors through a singular Sturm-Liouville operator whose natural Friedrichs realisation is solvable: after a compacton-adapted change of variable, the eigenvalue equation is of Jacobi-hypergeometric type, with two explicit spectral sequences, exactly one negative eigenvalue and a one-dimensional translation kernel. Numerical experiments connect these exact structures to approximation and dynamics. Measured Fourier tails and projection errors agree with the rates predicted by the free-boundary exponent, while padded Fourier-Galerkin time integration propagates the cusped waves coherently with small phase drift. A structure-adapted Jacobi-Galerkin diagnostic of the full generator finds no detectable real part in the tested truncated point spectra. The family therefore provides an explicit testbed in which geometric singularity, weak admissibility, spectral structure and numerical regularity of compactons can be studied within a single degenerate dispersive model.

</details>


### [2] [Discrete PT-symmetric solitons on branched lattices: Vertex states, stability, and vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00774)
*M. Akramov,A. Fayzullaev,O. Tojakhmadova,T. Akhmadjanov*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 研究了PT对称四臂星形图中的离散非线性薛定谔方程局域态，发现由分支几何支撑的固定拓扑荷离散涡旋态，并讨论其电路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分支结构引入了一维晶格所不具备的顶点自由度，从而从根本上改变非线性局域行为，因此需要探究分支几何对局域态、拓扑结构以及PT对称性的影响。

Method: 以PT对称四臂星形图为模型，采用离散非线性薛定谔方程描述；从反连续极限出发，通过延拓方法构造离散涡旋态，并分析其在平衡增益与损耗下的演化与稳定性。

Result: 识别出具有固定拓扑荷的离散涡旋态，该态由分支几何支撑；在平衡增益与损耗条件下演化为载流态，并呈现出常规分支所不具备的稳定性窗口。

Conclusion: 分支几何可支撑新型拓扑局域态并带来独特的稳定性特征，同时该模型具备用电路实现的可能。

Abstract: Branching fundamentally modifies nonlinear localization by introducing vertex degrees of freedom absent in one-dimensional lattices. We investigate localized states in a PT-symmetric four-edge star graph described by the discrete nonlinear Schrödinger equation. We identify a discrete vortex state with a fixed topological charge, supported by the branching geometry. Constructed by continuation from the anticontinuum limit, the vortex evolves into a current-carrying state in the presence of balanced gain and loss and exhibits stability windows absent for the conventional branches. A possible electric-circuit realization of the proposed model is discussed.

</details>


### [3] [The role of root allometry and root overlap in vegetation pattern formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01730)
*Jelle van der Voort,Ricardo Martinez-Garcia,Arjen Doelman*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该文从植被-土壤水显式模型出发，在快水动力学极限下消去水分变量，得到刻画根系异速生长与根系重叠竞争的非局部标量植被模型，并揭示两种可驱动图灵失稳的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部标量植被模型通常只用单个卷积项描述资源竞争，忽略了根系异速生长与根系重叠导致的地下竞争，可能遗漏真实的模式形成机制。

Method: 构建植物通过侧向扩展根系获取土壤水的空间显式双组分植被-水系统，用随地上生物量密度增大而扩展空间范围的相互作用核表示根系异速生长；在快水动力学极限下消去水分变量，得到非局部标量模型，并对其开展图灵失稳分析。

Result: 约化显示根系重叠自然产生双积分结构，而非通常的单卷积项；识别出两种图灵失稳机制：一是根系生长速率超过死亡率的机制，二是依赖竞争压力区形成的机制，即邻域较高生物量密度的累积竞争压力抑制植被生长。根系异速生长促进两种失稳机制，根系重叠则抑制竞争压力区的出现。

Conclusion: 研究植被格局形成时，根系异速生长与根系重叠均为不可忽略的关键特征。

Abstract: We investigate how root allometry and below-ground root competition due to root overlap affect the conditions for pattern formation in non-local scalar vegetation models. We start from a spatially explicit two-component vegetation-water system in which plants acquire soil water through their laterally extended root systems, with root allometry represented by an interaction kernel whose spatial extent increases with the above-ground biomass density. In the limit of fast water dynamics, we eliminate the water variable, resulting in a non-local scalar model for the vegetation dynamics. This reduction reveals that accounting for root overlap naturally leads to a double integral structure in the scalar model, in contrast to the single convolution term commonly used to model non-local competition for resources. For the resulting scalar model, we identify two distinct mechanisms that can drive Turing instabilities. The first instability mechanism requires root growth to outpace mortality, while the second mechanism relies on the development of competitive pressure zones, in which vegetation growth is impeded by the cumulative competitive pressure exerted by neighbouring areas with higher biomass density. Root allometry promotes the occurrence of both instability mechanisms, while root overlap inhibits the emergence of competitive pressure zones. Hence, our results show that both root allometry and root overlap are important features to consider when studying vegetation pattern formation.

</details>


### [4] [Stability and instability analysis of resonance-induced nonlinear bound states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01875)
*Jackson C. Turner,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文研究带紧支撑势的一维聚焦三次NLS-GP方程，建立了共振诱导非线性束缚态在分岔点附近轨道稳定性与不稳定性的精确判据，并给出共振频率足够小且本征模严格为正时稳定的推论，辅以数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明紧支撑势的反射系数在下半平面的纯虚零点（透射共振）或纯虚极点（散射共振）会产生"共振诱导非线性束缚态"，且此类态在严格正的L²激发阈值处分岔，与零点谱产生的、在零L²范数处分岔的通常非线性束缚态形成对比；但这些共振诱导态的动力学稳定性此前尚不清楚。

Method: 在分岔点附近对共振诱导非线性束缚态进行稳定性分析，建立轨道稳定与轨道不稳定的精确判据，并针对共振频率与本征模符号给出稳定性充分条件；同时开展数值模拟。

Result: 获得了共振诱导态在分岔点附近轨道稳定/不稳定的精确判据；推论表明，当（i）底层线性（散射或透射）共振频率足够小，且（ii）对应线性共振本征模严格为正时，共振诱导非线性态是轨道稳定的。数值模拟进一步探索了理论无法覆盖的区域，并刻画了稳定与不稳定共振诱导态附近初值的大时间动力学。

Conclusion: 共振诱导非线性束缚态的稳定性可由线性共振频率大小与本征模正性等条件精确判定，理论判据与数值观察共同刻画了这类阈上分岔态的动力学行为。

Abstract: We study the focusing one-dimensional cubic nonlinear Schrödinger / Gross--Pitaevskii equation (NLS-GP), where the potential of the underlying linear Schrödinger operator, $H_V=-\partial_x^2+V(x)$, is compactly supported. In \cite{turner2026resonance} the authors proved that purely imaginary zeros (transmission resonances) or purely imaginary poles in the lower half plane (scattering resonances) of a reflection coefficient of $H_V$ seed branches of {\it resonance-induced nonlinear bound states}, and that these states bifurcate at a strictly positive $L^2(\mathbb R)$ excitation threshold. This is in contrast to nonlinear bound states which bifurcate at zero $L^2$-norm from point spectra of $H_V$ (corresponding to poles in the upper half plane). In this paper we establish precise criteria for the nonlinear orbital stability and instability of the resonance-induced states near the bifurcation point. A corollary is that resonance-induced nonlinear states are orbitally stable if i) the underlying linear (scattering or transmission) resonance frequency is sufficiently small, and ii) the corresponding linear resonance eigenmode is strictly positive. Numerical simulations are presented to explore regimes not accessible to our theory, and to explore the large time dynamics for initial conditions near stable and unstable resonance-induced nonlinear bound states.

</details>

### [5] [High-Value Synthetic Supervision for Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of a Compact Japanese Speech Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00026)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 以182段合成日语音频微调1.47B音频模型，将护理交班语音映射为六字段结构化笔记；LoRA(r=16)以约0.85%可训练参数达到全量微调97.7%的聚合表现，但仅证明参数高效的能力获取，不涉及临床效度与真实语音迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实私有场景语音（如护理交班）难以采集与再分发，且紧凑模型通常依赖任务专属监督；因此需要一条可审计的合成数据流水线来支撑音频到结构化文本的能力获取研究。

Method: 构建可审计合成流水线，将日语护理交班音频直接映射为六字段结构化笔记；使用182段合成训练/开发片段，对1.47B音频模型进行全量微调与秩16 LoRA适配；在39段场景种子不相交的合成测试集上评测，采用模型评判的事实性召回指标，并记录训练参数遥测。

Result: 未适配模型事实性召回0.0500，全量微调0.8664，LoRA 0.8461；LoRA聚合表现达全量微调的97.7%，可训练参数12.4M（约占骨干0.85%）。两种适配相对同一基座均有大幅配对增益；全量微调与LoRA的区间跨越零点，且优化配置不同，故不能宣称二者等价。

Conclusion: 该结果属于参数高效的能力获取证据，而非设备性能证据：未测延迟、内存、能耗与实时因子。全部评测语音与目标均为合成，参考由模型生成，评判器未经校准。结论仅表明紧凑模型可从数百条来源可追溯的合成样本中习得狭窄的音频到结构化转换，不构成临床效度、真实语音迁移或优于清洁云端系统的证明。

Abstract: Private domain speech is difficult to collect and redistribute, while compact models need task-specific supervision. We study an auditable synthetic pipeline that maps Japanese care handoffs directly to six-field structured notes. Using 182 synthetic training and development clips, we adapt a 1.47B audio model by full fine-tuning and rank-16 LoRA. On a 39-clip scenario-seed-disjoint synthetic test, an unadapted model obtains a model-judged factuality-recall score of 0.0500, full tuning 0.8664, and LoRA 0.8461. LoRA reaches 97.7% of the full-tuning aggregate as a descriptive ratio while project telemetry reports 12.4M trainable parameters, about 0.85% of the backbone. Both adaptations show large paired gains over the same base; the full-versus-LoRA interval crosses zero, and differing optimization settings preclude an equivalence claim. This is a parameter-efficient capability-acquisition result, not a device-performance result: latency, memory, energy, and real-time factor were not measured. All evaluation speech and targets are synthetic, references are model-proposed, and the judge is uncalibrated. The evidence shows that a compact model can acquire a narrow audio-to-structure transformation from a few hundred provenance-linked synthetic examples; it does not establish clinical validity, real-speech transfer, or superiority to a clean cloud system.

</details>


### [6] [How Robust Are Neural Audio Codecs for African Speech? A Multi-Task Benchmark and the Limits of Perceptual Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00154)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文对七个开源神经音频编解码器在三种非洲语音数据集上进行鲁棒性基准评测，比较信号级指标与ASR、ASV下游任务表现，并提出基于LoRA的高效编解码器自适应方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经音频编解码器虽可实现低码率语音压缩与分词，但在带口音和多语言语音上的鲁棒性尚未得到充分评估，这限制了其在包容性场景中的部署。

Method: 在afrinames、afrispeech dialog、afrispeech multilingual三个非洲语音数据集上，对DAC、EnCodec、FocalCodec、LanguageCodec、SemantiCodec、UniCodec、WavTokenizer七个开源编解码器进行基准测试；报告NISQA、UTMOS、ViSQOL、STOI、F0-RMSE等信号级指标，并评估ASR与ASV下游任务；同时采用LoRA对编解码器进行参数高效自适应（约1–3%参数，约35小时非洲语音）。

Result: 四项发现：一、信号指标的下游有效性差异显著，基于参考的结构/可懂度指标（ViSQOL、STOI）和韵律误差（F0-RMSE）比神经MOS预测器（NISQA、UTMOS）更能可靠反映ASR与ASV退化；二、可懂度与说话人身份保持在不同架构间差异明显，身份排序依赖ASV后端；三、退化具有强域依赖性，在对话场景中最严重；四、退化部分可恢复，LoRA自适应可缩小压缩导致的WER差距，使识别性能接近未压缩基线。

Conclusion: 语音编解码器评估需要面向任务、具有领域代表性并考虑自适应，这是实现包容性部署的前提。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs enable low-bitrate speech compression and tokenization, yet their robustness on accented and multilingual speech remains under-evaluated. We benchmark seven open-source neural codecs (DAC, EnCodec, FocalCodec, LanguageCodec, SemantiCodec, UniCodec, WavTokenizer) on three African speech datasets afrinames, afrispeech dialog, afrispeech multilingual, reporting signal-level quality (NISQA, UTMOS, ViSQOL, STOI, F0-RMSE) alongside two downstream tasks: automatic speech recognition (ASR) and speaker verification (ASV). Our analysis yields four findings. First, signal metrics differ sharply in downstream validity: reference-based structural/intelligibility measures (ViSQOL, STOI) and prosodic error (F0-RMSE) track ASR and ASV degradation far more reliably than neural mean-opinion-score predictors (NISQA, UTMOS). Second, intelligibility and speaker-identity preservation diverge sharply across architectures, and the apparent identity ranking itself depends on the ASV backend. Third, degradation is strongly domain-dependent and largest for conversational dialog. Fourth, the resulting degradation is partly recoverable: parameter-efficient \emph{codec} adaptation (LoRA, $\sim$1--3\% of parameters) on roughly 35 hours of African speech reduces the compression-induced word-error-rate gap, recovering recognition toward the uncompressed baseline (developed in companion work). These results motivate task-aware, domain-representative, and adaptation-aware evaluation of speech codecs as a prerequisite for inclusive deployment.

</details>


### [7] [ProxyMOS: Label-Free Speech Quality Assessment by Multi-Teacher Distillation with Adaptive Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00419)
*Maxim Trokunov,Kirill Borodin,Nikita Vasiliev,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: ProxyMOS 将多个公开 MOS 预测器集成为一个更强模型，无需新主观标注。经基准评测与集成搜索，最优四模型路由集标注 807k 无标签语句训练 wav2vec 2.0 学生模型，在 URGENT 与 mos260 上超越最佳教师，并发布模型、ONNX 导出与 mos260 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工 MOS 采集成本高，而非侵入式 MOS 预测器一旦跨出训练域性能显著下降。论文旨在无需新增人工标签的前提下，将现有公开 MOS 预测器池整合为单一更强模型，以提升跨域鲁棒性。

Method: 对八个预测器与人工评分进行基准评测，选出信息量最高的五个进入子集搜索；搜索策略包括均匀、相关性加权、误差加权、MSE 优化及自适应逐句路由；以最佳路由四模型集成标注 807k 无标签语句，蒸馏训练 wav2vec 2.0 学生模型。

Result: URGENT 上学生模型 Spearman ρ=0.802，优于最佳教师 0.773；在新建俄语 TTS 基准 mos260（38 种合成条件、4,600 条语句）上，逐句 ρ=0.636（教师 0.613），逐条件 ρ=0.95，单次前向即匹配其路由集成。自适应路由是唯一在加入弱预测器时不退化、反而受益的规则。

Conclusion: ProxyMOS 证明通过自适应路由的预测器集成与知识蒸馏，可在无新人工标签条件下获得跨域更稳健的非侵入式 MOS 预测。模型、ONNX 导出与 mos260 基准均已发布。摘要约 950 字符，符合 arXiv 1,920 字符限制；ρ 符号在摘要页可正常渲染，如需避免数学符号可改用 rho。

Abstract: Human mean opinion scores (MOS) are costly to collect, and non-intrusive MOS predictors degrade sharply outside their training domain. ProxyMOS turns a pool of public MOS predictors into a single stronger model without new human labels. Eight predictors are benchmarked against human ratings; the five most informative enter a subset search under uniform, correlation-weighted, error-weighted, MSE-optimised and adaptive per-utterance routing; and the best routed four-model ensemble labels 807k unlabeled utterances that train a wav2vec 2.0 student. On URGENT the student reaches Spearman $ρ=0.802$ against $0.773$ for the best teacher. On mos260, a new Russian TTS benchmark of 4,600 utterances from 38 synthesis conditions, it reaches $ρ=0.636$ against $0.613$ per utterance and $0.95$ per condition, matching its own routed ensemble in one forward pass. Adaptive routing is the only rule that does not degrade when weak predictors are added. Model, ONNX exports and mos260 are released. It's about 950 characters; arXiv's limit is 1,920. I kept $ρ$ because arXiv renders it on the abstract page. If you'd rather avoid math, replace $ρ=0.802$ with rho = 0.802 and do the same for the other $...$ values.

</details>


### [8] [Collapse, Not Invariance: Diagnosing Auxiliary Objectives in Speech Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00539)
*Ksenia Lysikova,Kirill Borodin,Maxim Maslov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 论文比较7种辅助目标函数与交叉熵在语音防欺骗任务中的效果，发现没有任何辅助目标能在不同架构、语料和随机种子下稳定优于交叉熵，EER指标掩盖了嵌入空间的真实退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音防欺骗检测器在生成器、编解码器或信道变化时性能下降，常用辅助目标函数来塑造嵌入空间以提升鲁棒性。然而EER是纯排序指标，无法反映嵌入空间是否真正被改善，因此需要直接测量嵌入空间来验证辅助目标的有效性。

Method: 在5个语料库上进行113次实验，比较7种辅助目标与交叉熵；使用AASIST3的3个随机种子加4个预训练检测器，并直接测量24次AASIST3运行的嵌入空间。

Result: 原始增强位移使余弦一致性看似有效，但增益来自更小的空间而非更稳定的空间；按空间展布归一化后无配置能持续优于交叉熵。所有训练空间都被两类任务预期的单一决策轴主导，其训练集结构无法迁移；4次运行坍缩为近乎恒定的输出，位移会奖励这种情况而EER将其报告为低准确率。

Conclusion: 没有任何辅助目标能在架构、语料库和随机种子间保持对交叉熵的优势；EER作为评估指标掩盖了嵌入空间的退化和坍缩问题。

Abstract: Speech anti-spoofing countermeasures degrade when the generator, codec or channel changes, and a common remedy is an auxiliary objective that shapes the embedding space; whether it does is invisible to EER, a pure ranking metric. We compare seven such objectives with cross-entropy over 113 runs on five corpora, AASIST3 at three seeds plus four pre-trained detectors, and measure the embedding space of the 24 AASIST3 runs directly. Raw augmentation displacement makes cosine consistency look effective, but the gain is a smaller space, not a more stable one: normalised by the spread, no configuration consistently improves on cross-entropy. Every trained space is dominated by the single decision axis expected for two classes, whose training-set structure does not transfer, and four runs collapse to a near-constant output that displacement rewards and EER reports as poor accuracy. No auxiliary objective keeps an advantage over cross-entropy across architectures, corpora and seeds.

</details>


### [9] [PLACE: Positional Latent Adaptation via Conditioned Embeddings for Binaural Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00630)
*Tiernon Riesenmy,You Zhang,Gautam Bhattacharya,Andrea Fanelli*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: PLACE 通过将感知编码器特征、文本-视频对齐空间线索与条件依赖低秩变换引入 AudioX，实现了由文本、视频及可选音频提示驱动的任意到双耳音频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向任意多模态提示（文本、视频、可选音频）的双耳音频生成仍具挑战，需在保持预训练 any-to-audio 模型能力的同时增强空间一致性。

Method: 扩展 AudioX：以 Perception Encoder Core 特征增强视频条件；对齐文本与视频表示以导出空间线索；对生成潜变量施加条件依赖的低秩变换；用解码音频的双耳电平差与时间差目标监督适配器；在 MRSAudio 上训练。

Result: 在 FAIR-Play 上多数指标优于 ViSAGe；在 BEWO-1M Single Static 测试划分上 SpatialCLAP 优于 SpatialSonic；听众评测在视频到音频及分布外文本到音频生成中偏好 PLACE。

Conclusion: PLACE 支持灵活的多模态控制并提升空间一致性，验证了感知特征、空间对齐与低秩适配相结合的有效性。

Abstract: We present PLACE, a method that extends the pretrained any-to-audio model AudioX for binaural generation from arbitrary combinations of text, video, and optional audio prompts. PLACE augments video conditioning with Perception Encoder Core features, aligns text and video representations to derive spatial cues, and applies a conditioning-dependent low-rank transformation to the generated latent. The adapter is supervised via decoded-audio interaural level and time difference objectives. Trained on MRSAudio, PLACE improves most metrics over ViSAGe on FAIR-Play and achieves an improved SpatialCLAP score over SpatialSonic on the BEWO-1M Single Static test split. Listener evaluations favor PLACE for video-to-audio and out-of-distribution text-to-audio generation, demonstrating flexible multimodal control and improved spatial consistency.

</details>


### [10] [On Evaluating Quantum Kernel Robustness for Low-Resource Cross-Corpus Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00649)
*Lisan Al Amin,Lei Zhang,Vandana P. Janeja*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 在仅200个训练样本、4维PCA降维的严格低资源约束下，比较QSVM、SVM与MLP在跨语料合成语音检测中的表现；QSVM在ASVspoof 2019→ADD 2023的严重域偏移下显著优于MLP，但在近域迁移中并不一致占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成语音检测对音频安全至关重要，但标注数据稀缺、评测条件与训练分布不一致时性能会显著下降。作者希望探究在低资源与跨语料域偏移条件下，量子核方法与轻量神经模型是否具备竞争力。

Method: 使用冻结的wav2vec 2.0嵌入，并经主成分分析降至4维以匹配近期量子硬件的量子比特预算。在相同降维特征与200个训练样本的严格预算下，对比量子支持向量机（QSVM）、经典支持向量机（SVM）与多层感知机（MLP）。在ASVspoof 2019、ASVspoof 5、ADD 2023与In-the-Wild数据集上进行跨语料评测。

Result: 在从ASVspoof 2019迁移到ADD 2023的严重域偏移下，MLP退化至接近随机水平（AUC约50%，EER 50.0%）；QSVM仍保持有效判别能力（AUC 76.0%，EER 27.0%）。该优势并不跨迁移方向一致：以ADD 2023为训练集时，QSVM在三次迁移中有两次低于随机水平，而MLP表现更好。

Conclusion: 量子核方法在严重跨语料偏移与严格低资源约束下可具竞争力，但在近域迁移中并无一致优势。作者将结果解读为对分布偏移下量子核归纳偏置的实证刻画，而非量子优势的证据，因为4量子比特核可在经典硬件上精确模拟。

Abstract: Synthetic speech detection is critical for audio security, but performance can degrade when labeled data are scarce and evaluation conditions differ from training. This study examines quantum kernel methods and lightweight neural models for cross-corpus audio deepfake detection under limited training data. We compare a Quantum Support Vector Machine (QSVM), a classical support vector machine (SVM), and a multilayer perceptron (MLP), all trained on frozen wav2vec 2.0 embeddings using a strict budget of 200 training samples. To match the qubit budget of near-term quantum hardware, embeddings are reduced to four dimensions using principal component analysis, and all models use the same reduced features. Experiments on ASVspoof 2019, ASVspoof 5, the ADD 2023 Challenge, and the In-the-Wild dataset show that under severe domain shift from ASVspoof 2019 to ADD 2023, the MLP degrades to near-random performance, with an area under the curve of approximately 50% and an equal error rate of 50.0%. In contrast, the QSVM maintains meaningful discrimination, achieving an area under the curve of 76.0% and an equal error rate of 27.0%. This advantage is not consistent across transfer directions. When trained on ADD 2023, the QSVM falls below chance on two of three transfers, while the MLP performs better. These results suggest that quantum kernel methods can be competitive under severe cross-corpus shifts and strict low-resource constraints, but do not provide a consistent advantage under near-domain transfer. We interpret these findings as an empirical characterization of quantum kernel inductive bias under distribution shift, rather than evidence of quantum advantage, since the four-qubit kernel can be simulated exactly on classical hardware.

</details>


### [11] [Balalaika-Longform: A Russian Speech Corpus for Continuous Long-Form Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00658)
*Nikita Vasiliev,Kirill Borodin,Vasilii Kudryavtsev,Maxim Maslov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文发布Balalaika-Longform俄语长文本语音语料（189小时，30秒至15分钟连续单元），通过长短窗口微调对比，证明训练序列长度是长文本TTS稳定性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本合成需在长时生成中保持目标词完整，但句子级训练与评估会掩盖漏词和提前停止问题；同时缺乏同一音源下长短训练视图的可控对比资源，尤其针对俄语。

Method: 构建Balalaika-Longform语料：189小时俄语，连续单元30秒至15分钟，并提供匹配的短窗口以保证同一录音来源；评估保留每次合成尝试。在CosyVoice3、Qwen3-TTS、VoxCPM2、F5-TTS上分别用长/短视图微调，对50组未见音色的文本-音色对，在约75、300、1200词长度下进行连续合成测试。

Result: 1200词时，CosyVoice3的WER从短窗口微调的99.9%降至长目标微调的47.3%，使用带标点、格式对齐的转写进一步降至16.6%；成对区间显示长目标对Qwen3-TTS和VoxCPM2更优，对F5-TTS略有优势但其绝对WER仍高于90%。

Conclusion: 在固定连续生成协议下，训练序列长度本身即可显著影响长文本TTS的词保留能力；长单元训练与格式匹配转写是缓解漏词与提前停止的关键，且该结论依赖绝对WER而非仅相对比较。

Abstract: Long-form text-to-speech must retain requested words over extended generations, yet sentence-level training and evaluation can hide omissions and early stops. We introduce Balalaika-Longform, an open Russian corpus of 189 hours in continuous units of 30 seconds to 15 minutes. Long units and matched short windows support fine-tuning comparisons on the same source recordings, and the accompanying evaluation retains every synthesis attempt. We fine-tune CosyVoice3, Qwen3-TTS, VoxCPM2 and F5-TTS on either view and test continuous synthesis on 50 text-voice pairs with voices unseen in fine-tuning, at about 75, 300 and 1,200 words. At 1,200 words, CosyVoice3 WER falls from 99.9% after short-window fine-tuning to 47.3% with long targets, and to 16.6% with punctuated, format-matched transcripts; paired intervals favor long targets for Qwen3-TTS and VoxCPM2, and by a small margin for F5-TTS, whose absolute WER stays above 90%. The results isolate the effect of training sequence length under a fixed continuous-generation protocol.

</details>


### [12] [AnchorPrompt: Self-Distilled Soft Prompts for Robust Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00706)
*Pooneh Mousavi,Amir Ivry,Mirco Ravanelli,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 AnchorPrompt：冻结大音频语言模型，在解码器输入处学习一段提示向量，通过自蒸馏在多类音频/文本扰动下训练，实现扰动无关的推理与零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大音频语言模型对输入扰动（噪声、波形损坏、对抗注入）敏感，导致答案不一致与幻觉；同时希望避免扰动检测、无需微调模型参数即可提升鲁棒性。

Method: 保持模型冻结，仅在音频嵌入与问题嵌入之间、解码器输入端插入单块可学习提示向量；以干净录音上的预测作为可回答输入的目标，在音频证据不足时设定拒答目标，通过自蒸馏在多样音频与文本扰动上训练该向量；推理时扰动无关，无需先验检测扰动，可零样本迁移至未见失真。

Result: 在3个LALM、3个基准上评估：多数测试条件下答案一致性提升；9个模型-基准组合中6个干净准确率提升，其余最大仅下降1.2%；严重音频损坏下幻觉减少，干净音频上误拒答很少；一致性增益可迁移至选择项排列、混响等未见扰动。

Conclusion: AnchorPrompt 是一种高效、参数高效的适配方法，在不修改模型参数的前提下显著提升答案一致性与抗幻觉能力，并具备扰动无关与零样本迁移特性。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) are sensitive to input perturbations, such as noise, waveform corruption, and adversarial injections. We propose AnchorPrompt, an efficient adaptation method that keeps the model frozen and learns a single block of prompt vectors inserted at the decoder input, between the audio and question embeddings. We train these vectors through self-distillation over diverse audio and text perturbations. To improve answer consistency and mitigate hallucination, we use the model's prediction on the clean recording as the target for answerable inputs, and assign a refusal target when the audio lacks sufficient evidence to answer. Furthermore, AnchorPrompt is perturbation-agnostic at inference, requiring no prior detection of perturbations and enabling zero-shot transfer to unseen distortions. We evaluate three LALMs across three benchmarks and show that AnchorPrompt improves answer consistency in most tested conditions. Clean accuracy improves in six of nine model-benchmark pairs, with minimal impact on the remainder of 1.2% at most. Crucially, AnchorPrompt reduces hallucinations under severe audio corruption while keeping false refusals on clean audio rare. Finally, these consistency gains transfer to unseen perturbations, such as choice permutations and reverberation.

</details>


### [13] [Where the Body Keeps the Beat: Structured Motion Conditioning and Music Dynamics Supervision for Dance-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00726)
*Changchang Sun,Lu Cheng,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出Dyna2Music，一种潜在流匹配框架，通过结构化运动条件与音乐潜在动态显式监督，实现舞蹈到音乐生成，在节奏对齐与音频质量上优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有舞蹈到音乐（D2M）方法未显式区分不同身体部位与频段的运动线索，且标准流匹配缺乏对局部音乐潜在动态的专门监督目标，导致生成音乐的时序结构与舞蹈动作对齐不足。

Method: 在AIST++上进行实证分析，量化空间划分与频率分离对原始音乐-运动学节拍对齐及运动参考密度的影响，据此设计条件。Dyna2Music将关节速度分解为慢、快分量，并将部位级运动能量与预训练关节特征分层融合以条件化音乐生成。同时引入潜在动态一致性（LDC）辅助目标，对单步干净潜在估计与配对参考之间的相邻帧变化幅度进行匹配，不增加可训练参数或推理计算量，并支持变长音乐生成。

Result: 在AIST++与TikTok数据集上的实验表明，Dyna2Music在节奏对齐与音频质量方面优于代表性D2M基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的运动条件设计与潜在动态显式监督可有效提升D2M生成的节奏对齐与音频质量，且LDC以零额外参数与推理开销实现局部潜在动态监督，支持变长生成。

Abstract: Dance-to-music (D2M) generation aims to synthesize musically plausible soundtracks whose temporal structure aligns with a given dance performance. Representative D2M methods do not explicitly distinguish motion cues across body parts and frequency bands, while standard flow matching lacks a dedicated objective for supervising local music-latent dynamics. To address these issues, we propose Dyna2Music, a latent flow-matching framework that combines structured motion conditioning with explicit supervision of music-latent dynamics. An empirical analysis on AIST++ quantifies how spatial partitioning and frequency separation affect raw music-to-kinematic beat alignment and motion-reference density, informing the conditioning design. Accordingly, Dyna2Music decomposes joint velocities into slow and fast components and hierarchically fuses the resulting part-wise motion energy with pretrained joint features to condition music generation. To complement this representation, we introduce latent dynamics consistency (LDC), an auxiliary objective that matches adjacent-frame change magnitudes between a single-step clean-latent estimate and the paired reference. LDC makes local music-latent variation an explicit training target without adding trainable parameters or inference computation. Dyna2Music supports variable-length music generation, and experiments on AIST++ and TikTok demonstrate improved rhythmic alignment and audio quality over representative D2M baselines.

</details>


### [14] [RMS-AQA: A Two-Stage Spatial Audio Question Answering Benchmark for Real-World Domestic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00935)
*Peihao Chen,Qing Wang,Lichun Fan,Yufeng Hao,Zhifeng Kong,Mengyao Zhu,Hengyi Hong,Hang Chen,Hang Su,Yujie Jian,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shichao Hu,Jun Du,Jian Luan,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出RMS-AQA，一个面向真实家庭环境的空间音频问答（SAQA）基准，采用两阶段QA格式评估音频语言模型（ALM）先定位可听声事件、再基于定位进行复杂时空推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中的具身助手需要推断发生了什么、何时何地发生以及如何响应，现有基准难以全面评估音频语言模型在真实空间音频场景下的定位与时空推理能力。

Method: 构建RMS-AQA基准：采用两阶段问答格式，将真实一阶Ambisonics（FOA）录音与基于实测房间脉冲响应（RIR）生成的高保真合成数据结合以提升声学真实性；并提供一个轻量级空间插件，将FOA格式数据注入冻结的音频语言骨干网络。

Result: 实验表明，主要挑战来源于并发声源、远距离以及RIR合成与真实录音之间的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）域差距。

Conclusion: RMS-AQA为真实家庭环境的空间音频问答提供了评估框架，揭示当前音频语言模型在空间定位与时空推理上的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Embodied assistants in domestic environments must infer what happened, where and when it occurred, and how to respond. To address this, we introduce RMS-AQA, a spatial audio question answering (SAQA) benchmark for real-world domestic environments. The benchmark features a two-stage question-answering (QA) format to comprehensively assess the ability of audio-language models (ALMs) to first ground audible sound events and subsequently perform complex spatio-temporal reasoning based on that grounding. To maximize acoustic realism, our dataset combines authentic real-world first-order Ambisonics (FOA) recordings with high-fidelity synthetic data generated using measured room impulse responses (RIRs). Furthermore, we provide a lightweight spatial plug-in that injects FOA-format data into frozen audio-language backbones. Experimental results reveal that the primary challenges stem from concurrent sources, far distance, and sim-to-real domain gap between RIR-synthesized and authentic recordings.

</details>


### [15] [Toward Elastic Speech Inference: Training-Free Wake-Word Detection from Pretrained ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01182)
*Hwayeon Kim,Youngwon Choi,Hyeonyu Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 探索免微调条件下，利用共享预训练ASR骨干网络进行唤醒词检测，并验证SliceGPT的PCA结构化剪枝可提取紧凑编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需通过额外训练或任务专用模块，将预训练ASR模型适配到唤醒词检测等前端任务，成本较高且缺乏通用性。本文关注跨域、跨声学条件泛化，尝试在不做梯度微调的前提下复用ASR骨干。

Method: 以共享预训练ASR骨干直接用于唤醒词（WuW）检测，不进行梯度微调；采用SliceGPT的基于PCA的结构化剪枝方法，从编码器中提取紧凑编码器。实验模型为Parakeet-TDT-0.6B-v3与Moonshine-base。

Result: 在编码器通道维度压缩50%时，唤醒词检测性能保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 无需微调即可从预训练ASR模型中导出任务相关的紧凑编码器，为前端任务复用ASR骨干提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent ASR development has placed growing emphasis on generalization across diverse domains and acoustic conditions. Existing approaches typically adapt pretrained ASR models to front-end functions such as wake-up word (WuW) detection through additional training or task-specific modules. In this work, we explore the use of a shared pretrained ASR backbone for WuW detection without gradient-based fine-tuning and examine whether a compact encoder can be extracted using the PCA-based structured pruning approach of SliceGPT. Experiments with Parakeet-TDT-0.6B-v3 and Moonshine-base show that WuW detection performance remains relatively stable when the encoder channel dimension is reduced by 50%. These results suggest that task-relevant compact encoders can be derived from pretrained ASR models without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [16] [AudioJev: Direct Audio Decisions with Order-Calibrated Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01293)
*Sihan Lv,Zhen Li,Zhiqi Cao,Jinshan Zhang,Ying Li,Meng Xi,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: AudioJev 用单一全参数模型将波形、问题与候选选项直接映射为候选分布，并定义"顺序校准"（保持语义不变、仅置换选项顺序时答案概率应不变）。训练采用随机错排 SKL：对同一问题构造每个选项都换位的重排视图，监督两个答案并在施加对称 KL 惩罚前对齐两者分布。推理只需一次候选打分前向，无需校准头或顺序集成。三个训练种子下在 MMAU/MMAR 上达 68.88%/55.33% 平均准确率，随机顺序 SKL 相比单视图去除消融降低 43.8%/60.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频决策常依赖转录文本无法保留的证据（如音色、环境声、韵律），且概率估计易受答案选项排列顺序影响。已有方法多依赖转录中介或顺序集成/校准头，既丢失音频信息又增加推理开销，因此需要直接面向音频、且对选项顺序保持概率稳定的建模方式。

Method: 提出 AudioJev：一个共享的全参数模型，输入波形、问题与候选选项，直接输出候选分布。定义"顺序校准"为在保持语义的选项置换下维持答案概率不变。训练目标为随机错排 SKL（Random-derangement SKL）：为每个问题构造一个所有选项均改变位置的重排视图，对两个视图的答案均进行监督，先对齐两个分布，再施加对称 KL 散度惩罚。推理仅保留单次候选打分前向，不使用校准头或顺序集成。

Result: 在三个训练种子下，AudioJev 在完整 MMAU/MMAR 上取得 68.88%/55.33% 的平均准确率；随机顺序 SKL 相对单视图去除消融分别降低 43.8%/60.5%。同一模型可处理意图、环境声、音符属性、语音活动与对话转换等多类任务；成对去除消融与多顺序评估同时衡量预测准确率与概率稳定性。

Conclusion: AudioJev 建立了直接的音频接口，其校准目标作用于候选语义而非呈现位置，在无需额外校准模块或顺序集成的情况下兼顾准确率与概率稳定性。推理代码与模型权重已公开于 GitHub 与 HuggingFace。

Abstract: Audio decisions often depend on evidence that a transcript does not preserve, while their probability estimates can depend on how answer options are ordered. AudioJev maps a waveform, question and supplied alternatives directly to a candidate distribution through one shared full-parameter model. We define order calibration as preserving an answer's probability under meaning-preserving option permutations. Random-derangement SKL training pairs each question with a reordered view in which every alternative changes position, supervises both answers, and aligns the two distributions before applying a symmetric KL penalty. Inference retains a single candidate-scoring forward, with no calibration head or order ensemble. Across three training seeds, AudioJev reaches 68.88%/55.33% mean accuracy on complete MMAU/MMAR and reduces random-order SKL by 43.8%/60.5% relative to the single-view removal ablation. The same model handles intent, environmental sound, note properties, speech activity and conversational transitions. Paired removal ablations and multi-order evaluation measure predictive accuracy and probability stability together, establishing a direct audio interface whose calibration objective acts on candidate meaning rather than presentation position. Inference code and model weights are available at https://github.com/SihanLv/AudioJev-Inference and https://huggingface.co/shlv/AudioJev.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-Party Backchannel Prediction: a Diagnosis, a Benchmark, and a Ceiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01488)
*Mohammed Hafsati,Ahmed Loughzali*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于 AMI 语料库的多方对话 backchannel（反馈信号）预测基准，并发现现有二元对话模型零样本应用于会议音频时仅达随机水平，但重训练后性能提升，且对未见听者泛化差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 backchannel 预测研究几乎全部局限于二元（dyadic）对话，缺乏多方对话场景的基准与评估工具，且不清楚二元模型能否迁移到多方会议场景。

Method: 构建基于 AMI 语料库的多方基准：682 个 masked-listener 视图，来自 171 场会议、190 位说话者、18,697 个 backchannel 事件，并采用 person-disjoint 划分。评估方法包括：二元 SOTA 模型零样本迁移、冻结声学特征的线性探针、重训练预测器；并尝试容量削减、听者对抗训练、逐听者自适应、oracle 词汇条件化等缓解手段；同时设 within-model 控制对比 turn-onset 预测。

Result: 二元 SOTA 模型零样本在会议音频上仅达随机水平（AUROC 0.499）；冻结特征线性探针达 0.704，重训练升至 0.751。听者条件化对训练中见过的听者有效，但对未见听者无效，且该 gap 在多种缓解手段下依然存在。对抗训练仅去除部分说话者身份信息，更强去除反而损害预测。within-model 控制显示 turn-onset 预测可迁移至未见听者，而 backchannel 预测不能。个体间 backchannel 率差异约为 turn-onset 率差异的两倍。因 backchannel 仅占约 1% 帧，报告 AUROC 及 listener-active 区域的 event-F1。

Conclusion: 多方对话 backchannel 预测存在跨听者泛化难题，听者身份与有用线索相互纠缠；需区分个体差异并采用合适指标（AUROC + event-F1）。作者公开基准与评估工具。

Abstract: Backchannel prediction has been studied almost entirely in dyadic conversation. We introduce a multi-party benchmark based on the AMI corpus, comprising 682 masked-listener views from 171 meetings, 190 speakers, and 18,697 backchannel events, with a person-disjoint held-out split. A state-of-the-art dyadic model applied zero-shot to meeting audio performs at chance (AUROC 0.499); nevertheless, its frozen acoustic features remain informative: a linear probe reaches 0.704, and retraining the predictor raises performance to 0.751. Retraining reveals a second limitation. Listener conditioning improves prediction for listeners seen during training but not for unseen listeners, and the gap remains under capacity reduction, listener-adversarial training, per-listener adaptation, and oracle lexical conditioning. Adversarial training removes only part of the speaker-identity information, while stronger removal hurts prediction, suggesting that identity is entangled with cues that are useful for backchanneling. A within-model control helps explain this pattern: with the same features and data splits, turn-onset prediction transfers to unseen listeners, while backchannel prediction does not. Backchannel rates also vary about twice as much across individuals as turn-onset rates. Since backchannels occupy only about 1% of frames, frame-level F1 is strongly affected by the base rate. We therefore report AUROC alongside event-F1 on listener-active regions. We release the benchmark and evaluation tools at https://github.com/HafsatiMohammed/bc_multiparty_release.

</details>


### [18] [Q-SPT: Learnable Query-Based Compression for Low-Frame-Rate Speech Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01492)
*Jeeyoung Yun,Seohwan Yun,Sungwoong Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Q-SPT：低帧率双流语音分词器，用可学习查询压缩器分别处理语义与声学流，兼顾语言信息与声学细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经语音编解码器作为SLM分词器时，降低帧率能减少计算与内存开销，但难以同时保留语言信息和声学细节。现有基于规则压缩的方法存在缺陷：平均池化会丢弃语言信息，相似度合并用固定阈值作用于相邻帧相似度，并将边界套用于声学流。

Method: 提出Q-SPT，低帧率双流语音分词器，使用分离的、上下文感知的、可学习的基于查询的压缩器，分别针对语义和声学表示。固定速率的查询独立地将语义流和声学流作为独立的键值源进行注意力计算，通过两个分别学习的压缩器实现流特定的上下文感知聚合。此外，用自回归文本损失显式监督语义压缩器以保留语言信息。

Result: 在相同帧率下，Q-SPT在所评估的编解码器中取得最佳重建效果。在下游SLM中，取得最佳语音识别准确率和TTS感知质量，并具有有竞争力的可懂度。

Conclusion: Q-SPT通过可学习查询驱动的双流压缩机制，在低帧率下同时保持语言信息与声学细节，在下游语音识别和语音合成任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Neural speech codecs increasingly serve as tokenizers for speech language models (SLMs). Lowering the frame rate reduces the computational and memory costs of SLMs, but makes it difficult to preserve both linguistic information and acoustic detail. Existing approaches rely on rule-based compression: average pooling can discard linguistic information, whereas similarity-based merging uses a fixed threshold on adjacent-frame similarity and applies the resulting boundaries to the acoustic stream. We propose Q-SPT, a low-frame-rate dual-stream speech tokenizer with separate, context-aware, learnable query-based compressors specialized for semantic and acoustic representations. In particular, queries at a fixed rate independently attend to the semantic and acoustic streams as separate key-value sources, enabling stream-specific, context-aware aggregation through two separately learned compressors. In addition, an autoregressive text loss explicitly supervises the semantic compressor to preserve linguistic information. Experimental results show that Q-SPT achieves the best reconstruction among the evaluated codecs at the same frame rate. In downstream SLMs, it yields the best speech recognition accuracy and text-to-speech perceptual quality with competitive intelligibility.

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Decodability: Do Acoustic Factors Drive Predictions in Speech-Based Alzheimer's Assessment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01846)
*Serli Kopar,Alkis Koudounas,Roshan P. Rane,Sam Gijsen,Paula A. Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 语音AD检测中，SSL模型学到的声学表征不仅编码录音因素，还能系统性改变预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语音的阿尔茨海默病（AD）评估日益依赖自监督学习（SSL）预训练模型，这类模型直接从原始音频学习声学表征，从而暴露于录音相关因素（如噪声、混响）的影响。作者希望厘清：这些因素究竟只是被编码在SSL表征中，还是能系统性地改变AD预测。

Method: 使用ADReSSo数据集和三个大型SSL骨干网络，对纯参与者语音、非语音片段和完整录音施加受控的噪声与混响干预。结合逐层线性解码、输入空间与表征空间干预，以及几何对齐分析，来区分“声学可解码性”与“对AD预测的影响”。

Result: 受控声学干预在三个SSL骨干网络上均改变了AD预测。噪声在原始数据中虽未显示出显著诊断组差异，却产生了最强的干预效应。这些效应相对于分类器决策方向具有系统性结构，在留出测试集上可复现，且在反转表征空间干预方向时会随之反转。

Conclusion: 高预测性能与某一声学因素缺乏显著诊断组差异，均不足以证明模型稳健性；基于干预的稳健性测试应成为可信临床语音模型的标准流程。

Abstract: Speech-based Alzheimer's disease (AD) assessments increasingly rely on pretrained self-supervised learning (SSL) models that learn acoustic representations directly from raw audio, exposing the model to recording factors. We ask whether such factors are merely encoded in SSL representations or can systematically alter predictions. Using ADReSSo and three large SSL backbones, we apply controlled noise and reverberation interventions to participant-speech-only, non-speech, and full-recording audio. We combine layer-wise linear decoding, input- and representation-space interventions, and geometric alignment analysis to distinguish acoustic decodability from influence on AD prediction. Our results show that controlled acoustic interventions alter AD predictions across all three SSL backbones. Noise, despite showing no significant diagnostic-group difference in the original data, produces the strongest intervention effects. Importantly, these effects are systematically structured relative to the classifier's decision direction, replicate on the held-out test set and reverse when the representation-space intervention direction is reversed. Together, these findings show that high predictive performance and the absence of a significant diagnostic-group difference in a measured acoustic factor are not sufficient for robustness. We argue that intervention-based robustness tests should become standard for trustworthy clinical speech models.

</details>


### [20] [From Isolated Feature to Orbits: Discovering Music Concepts via Multi-SAE Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01864)
*Liwei Lin,Gus Xia*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究提出从"特征识别"转向"结构分析"，利用音高移调作为归纳偏置，通过多视角SAE对齐诱导有序轨道，从而在音乐基础模型中发现和弦、调性、旋律模式等结构化概念表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（如探针、稀疏自编码器SAE）主要关注在最小结构假设下识别孤立特征，但音乐中的许多概念（如和弦、调性）本质上是结构化关系集合（如和弦的12个移调、调内的自然音体系），而非孤立特征，因此需要从特征识别转向结构分析。

Method: 提出一个以音高移调为归纳偏置的框架，通过多视角SAE对齐诱导有序轨道。具体做法是生成音高移调的输入对，对齐其SAE表示，从而发现音高相关特征的结构化分组。

Result: 在两个最先进的音乐基础模型上，该方法恢复了对应于和弦、调性和旋律模式的轨道结构，且仅需极少的锚点示例即可解释整个概念族。

Conclusion: 音乐基础模型的内部表示确实以组织化结构形式涌现，而非孤立特征；从特征识别到结构分析的分析范式转换，为理解音乐基础模型内部机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: How can we understand what a music foundation model has learned \textit{internally}? Most interpretability approaches, such as probing and Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), focus on identifying individual features with minimal structural assumptions. We argue that many concepts are better understood as \textit{structured relations} rather than isolated features. This is especially prominent in music, where tonal structures are organized in the space of pitch and time. For example, concepts such as chords or keys are naturally expressed as structured sets (e.g., the 12 transpositions of a chord or the diatonic system within a key), rather than isolated features. In this study, \textbf{we shift from feature identification to structure-based analysis}, asking whether the learned inner representations of music foundation model emerge as organized structures over features. To this end, we introduce a framework that uses pitch transposition as an inductive bias to induce ordered orbits via multi-view SAE alignment. Concretely, we generate pitch-shifted input pairs and align their SAE representations to discover structured groups of pitch-related features. Experimental results show that this approach recovers orbit structures corresponding to chords, keys, and melodic patterns across two state-of-the-art music foundation models, while requiring only minimal grounding (e.g., a few anchor examples) to interpret entire concept families.

</details>


### [21] [LAST: Looped Audio Spectrogram Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01926)
*Haider Al-Tahan,Sean O'Brien,Anastasia Razdaibiedina,N. Apurva Ratan Murty*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 LAST 模型：先处理全部 token，再复用同一组 block 仅迭代精炼 class token，以极低额外算力换取精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加深 Transformer 虽能提升识别性能，但每增加一层都带来更多参数与计算开销，效率低下。作者希望让额外的处理聚焦于整合已计算出的特征，而非从头计算新特征。

Method: Looped Audio Spectrogram Transformer (LAST)：首先对所有 token 进行一次性处理，随后复用相同的 Transformer blocks，在固定的音频特征上仅对 class token 进行多轮精炼，使后续迭代的计算成本很低。

Result: 在 AudioSet 上，十轮 LAST 达到 0.345 mAP，相对十二层顺序 Transformer 提升 2.1%，同时参数减少 49.4%、MACs 减少 42%、实测吞吐提升 9.8%。独立训练的模型中将轮数从 2 增至 10 可持续提升精度，而计算量仅增加 1.2%。在时间掩蔽及多种听觉增强下鲁棒性更好，并在音乐、环境声与事件声分类任务上泛化更优。

Conclusion: 通过循环复用 block 仅精炼 class token，可在显著降低参数与计算量的同时超越更深的顺序 Transformer，并提升鲁棒性与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Increasing depth of transformer models improves recognition, but it comes at a substantial cost. Each additional layer requires more parameters, which makes the process computationally inefficient. We ask whether additional processing can focus on integrating features already computed. Looped Audio Spectrogram Transformer (LAST) first processes all tokens, then reuses the same blocks to refine only the class token over fixed audio features, thereby making later passes inexpensive. On AudioSet, ten-pass LAST achieves 0.345 mean average precision, exceeding a twelve-layer sequential transformer by 2.1% relative with 49.4% fewer parameters, 42% fewer multiply-accumulate operations, and 9.8% higher measured throughput. Across separately trained models, increasing the pass count from two to ten improves accuracy while adding only 1.2% computation. Further evaluations show improved robustness to temporal masking and various other auditory augmentations, with better generalization on classification tasks with music, environmental, and event sounds.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [22] [Finite Element Simulation of Microwave Technologies for PowderBased Volumetric Additive Manufacturing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00842)
*Brendan Mackey,Levi Smith,Bosco Yu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过有限元仿真探索基于微波的体增材制造(VAM)系统，兼容陶瓷、金属或复合粉末，采用线性谐振器、交叉干涉仪和边界加热器三种装置，证明可工程化热格栅实现选择性加热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体增材制造(VAM)系统局限于光聚合树脂，限制了其在各行业的通用性；因此需要探索能兼容陶瓷、金属、复合材料粉末的微波基VAM系统。

Method: 采用探索性有限元仿真，研究三种微波装置：线性谐振器、交叉干涉仪、边界加热器，针对陶瓷、金属或复合粉末原料；通过精确控制输入频率、功率和波导横模来工程化热格栅。

Result: 可精确控制输入频率、功率和波导横模来工程化热格栅，选择性地加热原料特定区域；所得格栅胞元尺寸约为1 mm，所需输入功率约为100 kW。

Conclusion: 随着微波技术在精度方面的进步，此类装置可开辟使用光学方法范围之外材料的VAM路径。

Abstract: Volumetric additive manufacturing (VAM) is a promising field of advanced manufacturing which reduces production times of conventional 3D printing while retaining application to complex geometries. Current VAM systems are limited to photo-polymerizing resins, which reduces the general applicability across industry. This work presents exploratory finite element simulations of microwave-based VAM systems compatible with ceramic, metal, or composite powder feedstock. The simulations explore three devices -- a linear resonator, a cross interferometer, and a boundary heater -- each with potential uses in manufacturing processes for one or multiple of the considered feedstock materials. We find that with precise control of input frequency, power, and waveguide transverse modes, thermal lattices can be engineered to selectively heat regions of the feedstock, allowing direct application to VAM. Resultant lattices have cellular dimensions on the order of 1 mm, and require input powers on the order of 100 kW. With advances in microwave technologies allowing for this precision, such devices could open pathways to VAM with materials currently outside the scope of optical methods.

</details>


### [23] [Upper Bounds on Efficiency of Pixel Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01107)
*Albert Salmi,Philipp del Hougne,Leo Lahti,Ville Viikari*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用半定松弛计算像素天线总效率的基本上限，并将该上限与遗传算法实现的实际解进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素天线效率优化属于含二值变量（像素导通/断开状态）的非凸问题，直接求解困难，且缺乏对可实现效率理论极限的明确认识，因此需要一种能给出效率上限的方法。

Method: 将"在二值像素状态下最大化天线总效率"的优化问题，通过半定松弛（SDR）转化为可求解的凸问题，从而得到总效率的上界；针对不同像素数量的天线分别计算该界，并用遗传算法（GA）求取实际可实现解以作对照。

Result: 给出了不同像素数目像素天线的总效率上界，并将其与遗传算法获得的可实现效率进行对比，显示二者之间的关系与差距。

Conclusion: 半定松弛能够为像素天线的总效率提供有效的基本界，可用于评估遗传算法等启发式优化结果接近理论极限的程度，为像素天线设计提供性能基准。

Abstract: This letter presents a method to compute fundamental bounds on the total efficiency of pixel antennas. Using semidefinite relaxation, we obtain an upper bound on the optimization problem of maximizing the antenna's total efficiency subject to binary-valued pixel states. The bounds are demonstrated for antennas with varying numbers of pixels and compared with realizable solutions obtained using the genetic algorithm.

</details>


### [24] [Correlation Imaging via Hybrid Entanglement between Microwave Photons and Surface Acoustic Wave Phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01252)
*Yu-Yuan Chen,Ling-An Wu,Yu-xi Liu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于微波光子-声子混合纠缠的相关成像方案，实现跨越约5个数量级的波长差与$10^{-8}$~$10^{6}$倍的空间缩放成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物理量子传感需要建立不同量子场之间的有效空间关联。微波光子与声子频率匹配但波长相差约五个数量级，有望提供一种独特纠缠资源，使探测与被读出的场可以分离。

Method: 利用超导量子电路产生微波光子与微波表面声波声子的混合纠缠；在匹配微波频率下，用光子场与被探测物体相互作用、通过声子芯片或光子读出器件进行相关成像。

Result: 实现两种具有巨大空间缩放能力的成像模式：将宏观物体轮廓缩小$10^{-8}$~$10^{-6}$倍投影到微观声子芯片上；反之，将微观物体轮廓放大$10^{4}$~$10^{6}$倍进入宏观光子读出器件。

Conclusion: 通过将自由空间长波长微波与片上短波长表面声波相连接，该相关成像方法为跨尺度量子传感提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Establishing effective spatial correlations between distinct quantum fields is essential for multi-physics quantum sensing.~We here propose a correlation imaging approach based on the hybrid entanglement between microwave photons and microwave surface acoustic wave phonons generated via a superconducting quantum circuit.~At matched microwave frequencies, the five order-of-magnitude wavelength disparity between photons and phonons leads to a unique quantum entanglement resource, which allows the optimal field for probing an object to be distinct from that used for readout.~This resource enables two imaging modalities with giant spatial scaling: a $10^{-8}-10^{-6}$-fold demagnification of macroscopic object profiles onto microscopic phononic chips, and conversely, a $10^{4}-10^{6}$-fold magnification of microscopic object profiles into macroscopic photonic readout devices. By interfacing free-space long-wavelength microwaves with on-chip short-wavelength surface acoustic waves, our correlation imaging approach provides a general framework for cross-scale quantum sensing.

</details>

*Guoshun Luo,Shichao Zhao,Xinyu Liu,Suguo Chen,Yue Wang,Ping Zhang,Zhigang Lu,Shaomeng Wang,Sunchao Huang,Yubin Gong*

TL;DR: 本文提出利用特征X射线产额非破坏性测定范德华薄膜厚度的方法，并建立了六阶多项式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征X射线产生通常用于X射线源和光-物质相互作用研究，作者希望拓展其在范德华薄膜厚度测量中的应用，实现非破坏性测厚。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种非破坏性测定范德华薄膜厚度的方法。

Abstract: Characteristic X ray generation is essential for producing X ray sources and for investigating light matter interactions. Here, we demonstrate that characteristic X ray generation can also be employed to determine the thickness of van der Waals thin films in a non destructive manner. Specifically, we establish a sixth order polynomial model that relates film thickness to the characteristic X ray yield. The model can both predict the X ray yield of graphite films at arbitrary thicknesses and infer the film thickness from a corresponding characteristic X ray yield. We validate the model by comparing its predicted X ray yields for graphite films with those obtained from Monte Carlo simulations. Additionally, we experimentally validated the reliability and feasibility of the proposed method for extracting graphite film thickness via X ray photon yield characterization. Our results provide a non destructive approach for determining the thickness of van der Waals thin films.

### [26] [When Intent Arrives Late: A Benchmark for Full-Duplex Speech Models under Delayed Intent Revelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00272)
*Yang Xiao,Tianyi Peng,Hanyu Meng,Ting Dang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 LateIntent-Bench，一个用于评测原生全双工语音模型在"意图延迟揭示"情形下安全行为的配对基准，并定义 Premature Response Rate (PRR) 度量响应是否早于意图揭示发生，发现现有仅基于完整请求的安全评测无法捕捉全双工模型新出现的响应时机模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生全双工语音模型可在用户说完之前就开始响应，使模型行为同时取决于最终话语内容与关键意图信息到达的时刻。然而现有基准主要评测"轮转机制（turn-taking mechanics）"，而安全评测通常假设生成响应前已观测到完整请求。这一评测缺口使得延迟揭示意图场景下的安全风险未被充分刻画，因此需要新的基准与度量。

Method: 提出 LateIntent-Bench：一个"配对（matched-pair）"基准，用于研究延迟意图揭示。每个配对共享一个歧义前缀，其后接良性或有害的续接内容，并通过受控停顿来延迟两条分支可被区分的时刻（延迟意图揭示）。定义 Premature Response Rate (PRR)，度量响应起始是否发生在意图揭示之前。通过联合评测有害与良性的参与/响应（harmful and benign engagement），区分"安全选择性变化"与"一般性响应能力下降"。共涵盖 3,136 个会话（sessions）。

Result: 在 3,136 个会话上，四个原生全双工模型在延迟意图下表现出不同的响应时机模式：三个模型在维持良性响应能力的同时增加了对有害分支的参与；一个模型对两个分支的参与度均下降。在意图揭示后插入 1.5s 静音，可使 PRR 保持在接近无停顿基线的水平，并显著减少有害参与度的变化。

Conclusion: 仅评测"完全明确的请求（fully specified requests）"无法捕捉全双工模型新出现的时序行为模式，说明在全双工模型中评估"延迟意图揭示"是必要的。代码即将公开。评测应同时考虑意图到达时机与响应起始时机，而非仅关注静态的完整请求安全性。

Abstract: Native full-duplex speech models can respond before a user finishes speaking, making the behavior of the model depend on both the final utterance and when intent-defining information arrives. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate turn-taking mechanics, whereas safety evaluations typically assume that the complete request is observed prior to response generation. We introduce LateIntent-Bench, a matched-pair benchmark for delayed intent revelation. A shared ambiguous prefix precedes either a benign or a harmful continuation, using a controlled pause to delay when the branches become distinguishable. We define the Premature Response Rate (PRR) to measure whether response onset precedes the revelation of intent. Joint evaluation of harmful and benign engagement distinguishes changes in safety selectivity from general losses in responsiveness. Across 3,136 sessions, four native full-duplex models exhibit distinct response-timing patterns under delayed intent. Three models show increased harmful engagement while maintaining benign responsiveness, whereas one loses engagement with both branches. Inserting a 1.5s silence after intent revelation keeps PRR near the no-pause baseline and substantially reduces changes in harmful engagement. These results demonstrate that evaluating fully specified requests alone fails to capture emerging timing patterns, highlighting the necessity of assessing delayed intent revelation in full-duplex models. The code will be publicly released soon.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-agent Auditory Scene Analysis: Improved Localization Speed and Robustness by Multi-beamformed Speech Quality Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00538)
*Caleb Rascon*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的优化机制，将实时听觉场景分析器（ASA）的质量优化从依赖单点质量估计改为基于一组覆盖多位置的质量估计集合，从而显著缩短优化时间、提高精度与稳定性，并在更低复杂度下实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体ASA通过智能体间通信形成反馈回路，在全局层面修正局部误差，实现鲁棒性并降低局部复杂度。但此前的优化速度很慢，原因在于其仅依赖一系列逐窗口波动较大的单点质量估计，导致搜索空间难以优化。

Method: 提出一种新的优化机制，不再依赖单点质量估计，而是利用覆盖一定位置范围的一组质量估计集合，从而提供更清晰的搜索空间视图并简化优化过程；该机制集成于多智能体ASA框架中，用于实时修正声源定位误差。

Result: 提出的ASA优化时间显著缩短，精度更高，在真实声学场景中修正更高水平定位误差时更稳定，且复杂度低于此前方案。唯一代价是质量估计智能体的响应时间增加，但完整ASA仍可实时运行。

Conclusion: 结果再次表明，将ASA建模为多智能体系统具有明显优势；用多位置质量估计集合替代单点估计可有效改善搜索空间的可优化性。

Abstract: A real-time auditory scene analyzer (ASA) aims to carry out the tasks of locating, separating and classifying the sound sources present in a given acoustic environment. Recently, an effort has been made into modelling an ASA as a multi-agent system, with each one of its agents performing one of the aforementioned tasks and communicating their results to the rest of their peer agents. These communication routes are used as feedback loops to fix local errors at a global level, providing robustness while reducing local complexity. An example of the benefits of this approach is the optimization of speech quality by correcting in real-time the estimated location of the speech source of interest. However, their optimization speed has been shown to be considerably slow. One possible reason is that it solely relies on a series of single quality estimations (provided by a reference-free quality estimator model) that vary considerably from one window to the next, which results in a difficult search space to optimize. In this work, a new optimization mechanism is proposed that instead relies on a series of sets of quality estimations over a range of locations, providing a clearer view of the search space, simplifying its optimization. The proposed ASA now has a considerably smaller optimization time, is more accurate, and is more stable when being evaluated in real-life acoustic scenarios to correct higher levels of localization errors, all while being less complex than previous efforts. The only trade-off is that there is an increase in the response time of the quality estimation agent, but the complete ASA is still able to run in real-time. The performance shown in this work again shows the benefits of modelling an ASA as a multi-agent system.

</details>


### [28] [End-to-End Historical Music Restoration in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00607)
*Steven Cho,Junghyun Koo,Raphael Lafargue,Tushar Dhyani,Eloi Moliner,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个监督式端到端管弦乐历史音乐修复(HMR)基准，通过更忠实地模拟历史录音退化链生成合成配对数据，并采用潜在流匹配模型进行修复，性能优于现有基线，同时发布了9.3小时无版权、非配对的古典音乐测试集及代码与音频示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史音乐修复(HMR)研究长期局限于超分辨率或独奏作品修复等受限问题，缺乏对多乐器管弦乐历史音乐的一般性修复探索。其根源在于该领域（20世纪初录音）不存在退化前的真实配对数据，使任务无监督且更具挑战性。

Method: 论文提出一种监督式端到端管弦乐HMR基准：(1) 比既有工作更忠实地模拟历史录音退化链，将管弦乐修复转化为可处理的监督问题；(2) 探索端到端生成式深度学习修复方法，采用潜在流匹配(latent flow-matching)模型在合成的配对数据上训练。此外，构建并发布9.3小时无版权、非配对的古典音乐测试集。

Result: 在合成配对数据上训练的潜在流匹配模型在侵入式、非侵入式以及主观评价上均优于现有HMR基线。

Conclusion: 该工作通过忠实模拟退化链与生成式建模，将管弦乐历史音乐修复确立为可监督学习的任务，并提供了基准、数据集、代码与音频示例，推动该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Historical music restoration (HMR) has almost exclusively focused on constrained problems such as Super-Resolution or the restoration of solo pieces, under-exploring the general task of restoring orchestral historical music, which has multiple instruments. This under-exploration is largely because the HMR domain, early-20th-century recordings, has no pre-degradation ground-truth pairs, making the restoration task unsupervised and more challenging. This paper presents a supervised end-to-end orchestral HMR benchmark by exploring both the synthetic degradation functions and the end-to-end generative deep-learning restoration methods. We simulate the historical recording degradation chain more faithfully than prior work, which makes orchestral restoration into a tractable supervised problem. A latent flow-matching model trained on the resulting synthetic pairs outperforms existing HMR baselines on intrusive, non-intrusive, and subjective evaluations. We also curate and release a 9.3-hour license-free, unpaired, historical classical-music test set, along with code and audio demos.

</details>


### [29] [Interpretable Destination-Aware synthesizer Modulation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00642)
*David Liu,Giulio Cengarle,David Cooper,Mark Vinton,Haici Yang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出可解释的、以调制目标为导向的调制与波形恢复流程，从干净音频中恢复可迁移至现代合成器的调制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成器编程（尤其是调制配置）极具挑战性；已有工作虽能通过曲线匹配从音频重建调制，但恢复的参数往往无法迁移到现代合成器，实用性受限。

Method: 构建可微分合成器作为训练支撑，引入噪声振荡器并支持比以往更多的调制选项；流程分三步：先识别被调制的目标（destination），再恢复每个目标对应的LFO波形，最后估计振荡器波形以匹配源音色；同时系统探索感知损失与对抗训练，提出Gammatone损失与CQT判别器。

Result: 客观与主观评测表明：正确预测调制目标是关键；模型在聚焦调制的逆向合成任务上表现优异；Gammatone损失与CQT判别器在感知质量上显著优于传统MSS损失。

Conclusion: 以目标为导向的可解释建模是解决调制逆向合成与参数可迁移性的有效途径，配合感知对齐的损失设计可显著提升重建音质。

Abstract: Synthesizer programming is a challenging task, particularly in configuring modulation: the time-varying control of parameters such as pitch, filter cutoff, and oscillator level by modulators such as Low-Frequency Oscillators (LFOs). Prior work has shown that modulation can be reconstructed from the clean audio through curve matching, yet the recovered parameters are often untransferable to modern synthesizers. We present an interpretable destination-aware modulation and waveform recovery pipeline that mirrors how musicians often recreate sounds on a synthesizer. First, the system identifies the modulated destinations; then it recovers the LFO shape associated with each destination, and estimates the oscillator waveform to match the source timbre. The training is supported by a differentiable synthesizer that incorporates a noise oscillator, and more modulation options than previous work. We carefully train the models for optimal perceptual quality, with extensive exploration of perceptual losses and adversarial training. Through objective and subjective evaluation, we show that predicting the destinations correctly is essential, our model excels in the modulation focused inverse synthesis tasks, and our Gammatone loss and CQT discriminator significantly outperform the traditionally-used MSS loss in improving perceptual quality. We provide audio samples from the subjective test.

</details>


### [30] [Silence-the-Mimic: Accelerating Imperceptible Perturbation Generation Against Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00662)
*Runqiu Xu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出STM，在频域生成受心理声学掩蔽约束的对抗扰动，实现对VC/TTS语音克隆的快速保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络驱动的语音转换与TTS模型可用极少数据实现逼真语音克隆，带来说话人身份被未授权克隆的隐私与安全风险；现有不可感知对抗保护方法依赖对超参敏感的质量控制损失，且优化耗时长、计算代价高。

Method: 提出一种快速保护方法STM：在频域生成感知受限扰动，并基于心理声学掩蔽约束；在对抗训练过程中严格施加可感知性边界，无需迭代式质量平衡，显著降低计算开销。

Result: 在多个先进VC与TTS模型上实验表明，STM取得相当或更优的保护性能，同时具备显著更好的感知质量，相比现有白盒基线最高实现45.3倍加速。

Conclusion: 频域扰动结合感知约束是一种实用且有效的防语音克隆保护范式。

Abstract: Deep neural network-based Voice Conversion (VC) and Text-to-Speech (TTS) models have rapidly advanced, enabling realistic voice cloning with minimal input data. Such capabilities raise serious concerns over unauthorized cloning of speaker identities and the associated privacy and security risks. Current imperceptible adversarial protection methods rely on quality control losses that are highly sensitive to hyperparameter tuning and computationally expensive due to lengthy optimization. To address these limitations, we propose a fast protection method that generates perceptually constrained perturbations in the frequency domain under a psychoacoustic masking-based constraint. Our approach strictly enforces perceptibility bounds during adversarial training, eliminating the need for iterative quality balancing and significantly reducing computational cost. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art VC and TTS models show that STM achieves competitive or superior protection performance with substantially better perceptual quality and up to $45.3\times$ speedup over existing white-box baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of frequency-domain perturbations with perceptual constraints as a practical paradigm for protecting against voice cloning.

</details>


### [31] [Frequency-Weighted Soft-Constrained Spatially Selective Active Noise Control for Open-Fitting Hearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00721)
*Tong Xiao,Reinhild Roden,Matthias Blau,Simon Doclo*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文将软约束空间选择性主动噪声控制（SSANC）从单一标量权衡参数推广到频率相关加权，并比较了SII、MIRS、LTASS及信号相关oracle四种加权方式。结果表明LTASS和oracle在语音可懂度与质量上改善最大且失真更低，LTASS无需干净目标语音即可接近oracle性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有软约束SSANC使用一个标量权衡参数，对所有频率统一处理，难以在噪声抑制与目标语音保留之间实现频率自适应的灵活折中。

Method: 在保持低延迟时域主动控制的前提下，引入频率相关加权于目标语音保留误差，比较基于SII、MIRS、LTASS及信号相关oracle的加权与标量加权，并使用多说话人场景下的实测声学脉冲响应进行验证。

Result: LTASS与oracle加权带来最大的语音可懂度提升，并在显著更低的失真水平下获得与SII、MIRS加权相当的语音质量提升。

Conclusion: oracle加权总体感知折中最佳，但LTASS加权无需干净目标语音信号即可提供可比的感知折中，因而更具实用价值。

Abstract: Recently, soft-constrained spatially selective active noise control (SSANC) for open-fitting hearables has been proposed to enable a flexible trade-off between noise reduction and desired-speech preservation using a scalar trade-off parameter across all frequencies. This paper generalizes soft-constrained SSANC by introducing frequency-dependent weighting of the desired-speech preservation error while maintaining low-delay time-domain active control. Frequency-dependent weightings based on the speech intelligibility index (SII), the modified intermediate reference system (MIRS), the long-term average speech spectrum (LTASS), and a signal-dependent oracle are compared with scalar weighting using measured acoustic impulse responses in a multi-speaker scenario. The results show that LTASS and oracle weightings provide the largest speech-intelligibility improvements and comparable speech-quality improvements at substantially lower distortion levels than those obtained with SII and MIRS weightings. While the oracle weighting provides the best overall perceptual trade-off, LTASS weighting provides a comparable perceptual trade-off without requiring the clean desired-speech signal.

</details>


### [32] [Articulatory Source-Filter TTS: Physically Grounded Control through Vocal Tract Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00735)
*Jesuraj Bandekar,Shinji Watanabe,Prasanta Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种基于发音运动学的可控源-滤波TTS架构，通过AAI模型生成运动学伪轨迹以条件化滤波响应，并用OT-CFM精化声源，实现声源-滤波解耦及细粒度可控合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经TTS声学保真度高但为黑箱，缺乏对声道滤波器的可解释控制。作者希望引入发音运动学先验，实现显式、可解释且细粒度的语音合成控制。

Method: 基于源-滤波框架：1）用大规模预训练表征增强的声学-发音反演（AAI）模型，为大规摸TTS语料生成运动学伪轨迹；2）轨迹条件化滤波响应，预测的基频与能量轮廓参数化声门声源；3）声源与滤波独立预测，声源经最优传输条件流匹配（OT-CFM）模块精化后与滤波重组为最终频谱图。

Result: 在可懂度与自然度上与同规模基线相当，仅以适度频谱保真度代价换取显式控制；评估显示清晰的源-滤波解耦，支持稳定的韵律缩放与跨说话人源/滤波重组（F0随源说话人、声道特征随滤波说话人）；直接操纵发音轨迹可实现口音修改等细粒度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了发音运动学驱动的源-滤波TTS在保持竞争力的合成质量同时，提供可解释、可解耦、可细粒度操控的合成控制，为可解释语音合成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Modern neural text-to-speech (TTS) systems achieve remarkable acoustic fidelity but act as black boxes, offering little interpretable control over the vocal tract filter. We propose a controllable source-filter TTS architecture grounded in articulatory kinematics. An Acoustic-to-Articulatory Inversion (AAI) model, enhanced by large-scale pretrained representations, generates kinematic pseudo-trajectories for a large TTS corpus. These trajectories condition the filter response, while predicted pitch and energy contours parameterise the glottal source. Source and filter are predicted independently, and the source is refined by an Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching (OT-CFM) module before recombination into the final spectrogram. Our model achieves intelligibility and naturalness competitive with similarly sized baselines, with only a modest spectral fidelity cost in exchange for explicit control. Evaluations reveal clear source-filter disentanglement, enabling stable prosodic scaling and cross-speaker source/filter recombination, where F0 remains tied to the source speaker while vocal-tract characteristics follow the filter speaker. Finally, direct manipulation of articulatory trajectories enables fine-grained control, such as accent modification, offering a new direction for interpretable speech synthesis. Audio samples: https://coding-phoenix-12.github.io/ArticulatorySFTTS/

</details>


### [33] [MAV-C: A Framework for the Joint Objective Estimation of Audio-Visual Complexity in Immersive Virtual Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00754)
*Luca Resti,Amelia Gully,Michael McLoughlin,Gavin Kearney,Alena Denisova*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出无参考视听复杂度联合估计框架MAV-C，融合熵基音频特征与视觉特征输出连续复杂度分数，并在合成与自然语料上验证其效度与敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视听复杂度缺乏统一、无需参考信号的联合客观度量，现有方法多分离处理音频与视觉模态，难以刻画二者独立且互补的信号维度。

Method: MAV-C提取熵基音频特征（时间、频谱、空间）与视觉特征（Sobel梯度幅值、色度独特性、光流），经参数化融合阶段产生联合复杂度分数CAV(t)∈[0,1]，并在受控合成语料SYN与SAFEPLAY-X的自然游戏语料GAM（60段）上验证，采用OFAT敏感性分析。

Result: SYN上音频分CA与视觉分CV对相反模态变化不敏感（CoV<0.003），CAV跨参数组合覆盖[0.00,0.90]，单轴特征扫描呈单调轨迹（Spearman达0.995）；GAM上CV在内容类别间差异显著（Kruskal-Wallis p=0.021）而CA不显著，两子分不相关（r=0.03）；敏感性分析揭示两层参数层级，模态平衡wa与视觉正则v最显著。

Conclusion: MAV-C在合成与自然语料上均表现出良好效度与模态独立性，但完整主观校准留待未来工作。

Abstract: We present the Motion-Aware Audio-Visual Complexity Metric (MAV-C), a reference-free framework for the joint objective estimation of audio-visual complexity. The metric combines entropy-based audio features (temporal, spectral, and spatial) with visual features (Sobel gradient magnitude, chromatic uniqueness, and optical flow) via a parametric fusion stage, producing a continuous joint complexity score CAV (t) [0,1]. We validate MAV-C on two datasets: a controlled synthetic corpus (SYN) of stimuli with known signal characteristics and a naturalistic gameplay corpus (GAM) of 60 clips drawn from the SAFEPLAY-X dataset. On SYN, the metric exhibits strong validity: the audio score CA and visual score CV are each insensitive to changes in the opposite modality (CoV < 0.003), the joint score CAV spans [0.00,0.90] across all parameter combinations, and single-axis feature sweeps produce monotone trajectories (Spearman up to 0.995). On GAM, CV differs significantly across content categories (Kruskal-Wallis p = 0.021) while CA does not, and the two sub-scores are uncorrelated (r = 0.03), confirming they operate on independent signal dimensions. OFAT sensitivity analysis identifies a two-tier parameter hierarchy, with modality balance (wa) and visual regularization (v) as most significant tunable parameters. Full subjective calibration is planned as future work.

</details>

*Yuxin Li,Yifei Li,Yi-Wen Chao,Xiangyu Zhang,Eng Siong Chng,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出ParaCalib框架，通过语义校准副语言行为，将声学线索置于话语级语义语境与交际功能中解读，在DAIC-WOZ与MODMA上取得最高平均Macro-F1（71.9%与90.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音抑郁检测方法将声学线索视为与语境无关的标记，难以区分"声音形式"与其依赖语境的交际功能，导致副语言信号的解释力受限。
