Result: 在容噪信道下：若数据库规模为10^4比特、数据库隐私为10^-6，约一千量子比特的块即可满足应用规定测量的用户需求；对单光子精确成立，在容噪信道上含小的多光子项。数据库隐私可扩展至诱骗态弱相干脉冲；仿真表明所给界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: 在NISQ设备的严格内存限制下，信息论安全SPIR是可行的；其核心机制是无歧义判别、联合随机置换分块与哈希奇偶校验，且硬件开销仅需在BB84设备上增加一个被动线性光学测量模块。认证任意无记忆用户与置换承诺会带来更大的辅助传输开销，需用有限密钥进行量化。

Abstract: Symmetric private information retrieval (SPIR) lets an inquirer obtain one specific bit only, without leaking any information about its index to the database owner. With a single server, this is impossible for parties with unrestricted quantum power. We show that it becomes possible, with information-theoretic security, when the server and the user hold only noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices without long-term quantum memory and measure each qubit on arrival, while the eavesdropper remains all-powerful; this is a stricter form of the memory restriction of the bounded- and noisy-quantum-storage models. The server sends BB84 states one way; the user discriminates them unambiguously and so knows a random subset of the bits. After a joint random permutation into blocks, the user announces the conclusive locations of one privately chosen block, and the server hashes and takes a random parity of these locations in every block; the permutation makes it exponentially unlikely that one set of locations fits two blocks. We prove secrecy against the eavesdropper, database privacy through an explicit size rule for the announced set, and user privacy against a server who prepares the prescribed BB84 states and announces truthfully, exactly for single photons and up to a small multi-photon term over a tolerated noise channel. The only addition to BB84 equipment is a passive linear-optical measurement with one ancillary mode. For a $10^{4}$-bit database and database privacy $10^{-6}$, blocks of about a thousand qubits suffice over a tolerated noise channel for a user who applies the prescribed measurement; certifying an arbitrary memoryless user and committing to the permutation need larger auxiliary transmissions, which we quantify with finite keys. Database privacy extends to decoy-state weak coherent pulses, and simulations show that the bound is nearly tight.

</details>


### [175] [Quantum state preparation for weighted d-DNNF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02094)
*Steef Hegeman,Joon Hyung Lee,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态制备问题，证明对由带权 d-DNNF（确定性、可分解伪布尔电路）描述的量子态，可在近似线性时间内构造出计算该态的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备问题要求给定量子态的描述后，高效生成计算该态的量子电路。一般量子态电路规模可能极小，而经典可高效处理的状态描述（如张量网络、电路类）与量子电路之间存在鸿沟，作者希望找到一类经典可高效处理的表示，使量子态制备也能高效完成。

Method: 作者针对带权 d-DNNF（deterministic、decomposable 伪布尔电路）描述的量子态，利用其确定性与可分解性的结构性质，设计算法将电路中的每个门/节点逐步映射为量子操作（受控门、旋转等），从而自底向上直接合成量子电路。

Result: 证明若量子态由带权 d-DNNF 描述，则可在（关于输入规模）线性时间内、除复数算术开销外，构造出计算该量子态的量子电路。

Conclusion: 带权 d-DNNF 是量子态制备的一类可高效处理的经典描述：量子态制备可从该类电路描述线性时间归约为电路合成，表明部分经典可高效表示的状态也能高效制备。

Abstract: The quantum state preparation problem is to, given a description of a quantum state, efficiently generate a quantum circuit computing the state. We show that for quantum states described by weighted d-DNNF (deterministic, decomposable pseudo-Boolean circuits) a quantum circuit computing the state can be obtained in linear time up to complex arithmetic.

</details>


### [176] [A No-Go Theorem for Order-Two Clifford Electric-Magnetic Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02097)
*Shunta Takahashi,Zhi Li,Beni Yoshida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了toric code中电磁对偶的微观Clifford实现是否存在二阶操作：证明对$\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code任何保局域Clifford实现电-磁交换都不能是二阶的；对$\mathbb{Z}_N$情形，奇数$N$存在二阶实现，偶数$N$不可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁对偶在任意子类型层面是二阶的，并在编码量子信息上表现为Hadamard型逻辑变换。作者追问这种对偶能否同样在微观上实现为二阶Clifford操作，即群律是否能忠实提升到微观Clifford实现。

Method: 对$\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code，给出完全一般的证明，证明任何保局域Clifford的电-磁交换实现都不可能是二阶的；证明不依赖平移对称性、受限系统尺寸族，也不依赖顶点与面稳定子之间的固定配对。几何上，证明通过局域模拟电弦与磁弦不可避免的交叉，类似于不可定向流形中的交叉帽。进一步将问题推广到$\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code进行分析。

Result: 对$\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code，任何保局域Clifford实现电-磁交换都不能具有二阶。对$\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code，奇数$N$存在二阶Clifford实现，而偶数$N$则不可能。

Conclusion: 涌现的电磁对偶的群律未必忠实提升为其微观Clifford实现；即任意子层面的二阶对偶对称性与微观Clifford实现的阶数可以不一致。

Abstract: Electromagnetic duality in the toric code has order two at the level of anyon types and acts as a Hadamard-type logical transformation on the encoded quantum information. Here, we ask whether it can likewise be realized microscopically as an order-two Clifford operation. For the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code, we prove that any locality-preserving Clifford realization of electric-magnetic exchange cannot have order two. Our proof is fully general and requires neither translation symmetry with restricted families of system sizes nor a fixed pairing between vertex and plaquette stabilizers. Geometrically, the proof locally emulates the unavoidable crossing between electric and magnetic strings, similar to a cross-cap in a non-orientable manifold. Extending the problem to the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code, we find that an order-two Clifford realization exists for odd $N$, whereas it is impossible for even $N$. Our results show that the group law of an emergent electromagnetic duality need not lift faithfully to its microscopic Clifford realization.

</details>


### [177] [An exponential separation between entanglement-assisted and unassisted one-way quantum communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02099)
*Ryan Anselm,Srijita Kundu,Olivier Lalonde,Ashwin Nayak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明存在全布尔函数在单向通信模型下，有纠缠时经典通信量为O(log n)，无纠缠时量子通信量需Ω(n^{1/3})，从而首次实现全布尔函数的指数分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信复杂度中一个长期未解的问题是：是否存在某任务在有纠缠时只需少量通信、无纠缠时却需要多得多的量子通信。此前仅在关系型问题和部分函数（同步消息传递模型）中已知此类分离，全布尔函数是否存在此类分离仍未解决。

Method: 构造一族全布尔函数f_n:{0,1}^n×{0,1}^n→{0,1}，该函数是子群成员问题（subgroup membership problem）的特例。分别分析其在单向通信模型下、有预共享纠缠时的经典通信上界，以及无纠缠时的量子通信下界。

Result: 该函数在有预共享纠缠时可用O(log n)比特单向经典通信计算，而在无纠缠时需要Ω(n^{1/3})量子比特的单向量子通信，实现指数分离。

Conclusion: 首次在全布尔函数上实现了有纠缠经典通信与无纠缠量子通信之间的指数分离，解决了量子通信复杂度中的这一长期开放问题。

Abstract: A longstanding question in quantum communication complexity is whether some task can be accomplished with a small amount of communication in the presence of entanglement, yet require much more quantum communication in the absence of entanglement. Separations of this nature were previously known for relational problems and, in the simultaneous message passing model, for partial functions. But it has remained unresolved whether any such separation exists for a total Boolean function. We resolve this question with an exponential separation in the one-way setting: we exhibit a family of total Boolean functions $f_n\colon \{0,1\}^n \times \{0,1\}^n \to \{0,1\}$ that can be computed with $O(\log n)$ bits of one-way classical communication given prior entanglement, but that require $Ω(n^{1/3})$ qubits of one-way quantum communication without entanglement. Our function is a special case of the subgroup membership problem, first studied in the communication setting by Aaronson, Le Gall, Russell, and Tani.

</details>


### [178] [On the pseudorandomness of simple quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02100)
*Jesko Dujmovic,Jonas Haferkamp,Alexander Poremba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究局域随机可逆操作的全局伪随机性问题，给出量子幺正类比下的两个否定性结果：幺正设计（统计矩匹配）一般不能推出伪随机性，并用反例反驳了置换情形HMMR猜想的幺正类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers (1996) 猜想：反复复合局域随机可逆操作可产生与随机置换不可区分的全局置换。作者希望通过对该问题的量子幺正类比研究，为这一长期猜想提供新进展，并厘清"简单过程为何能看起来高度复杂"这一核心问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为量子幺正类比：考察由单/双比特门分布经 T 步独立复合得到的 n 比特幺正系综。第一项结果构造对每个固定 t 存在高效可采样分布族 {ν_n}，使系综成为近似幺正 t-设计（误差 exp(-Ω(log²n))），但存在高效量子算法用 O_t(log²n) 次查询将其与随机区分；第二项结果给出在多项式有界矩上与伪随机幺正的更强分离，但其反例依赖高度结构化的系综，与简单的局域随机游走形成对比。

Result: 第一，统计矩匹配（幺正设计）一般不足以推出伪随机性，即使对最简单的量子过程亦然；这反驳了Hoory--Magen--Myers--Rackoff猜想(ICALP '04)的幺正类比（对置换）。第二，在多项式有界矩上获得幺正设计与伪随机幺正之间更强的分离，但所需反例为高度结构化系综。这提示用幺正设计建模黑洞物理中的信息扰乱需谨慎，因为即使最大程度扰乱的系统也可能留有可被高效实验探测的结构。

Conclusion: 基于上述否定性结果，作者提出新的猜想，刻画伪随机性在简单量子过程（如随机量子电路）中如何可能涌现，为Gowers猜想及信息扰乱模型研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Can simple processes appear highly complex? Gowers (Comb. Prob. Comp. '96) conjectured that repeatedly composing local random reversible operations can yield global permutations that are indistinguishable from random. In this work, we study the unitary quantum analog of this question, in an attempt to make new progress on this longstanding conjecture.
  Our first result shows that statistical moment matching in the form of unitary designs does not generically lead to pseudorandomness---even for the simplest quantum processes: for every fixed $t$, we give an efficiently samplable family $\{ν_n\}_n$ of distributions on one- and two-qubit gates such that, after $T=O_t(n^2\log^2 n)$ independent steps, the resulting $n$-qubit ensemble is an approximate unitary $t$-design with negligible error $\exp(-Ω(\log^2 n))$, yet an efficient quantum algorithm distinguishes it from random using only $O_t(\log^2 n)$ queries. This refutes the unitary analog of the Hoory--Magen--Myers--Rackoff conjecture (ICALP '04) for permutations.
  Our second result is a stronger separation between unitary designs and pseudorandom unitaries at polynomially bounded moments; our counterexample, however, requires highly structured ensembles, in contrast with the simple local walks from before. This suggests caution when using unitary designs to model information scrambling in black-hole physics, as even maximally scrambled systems can exhibit structure which is accessible to efficient experiments. Motivated by these findings, we then propose new conjectures for how pseudorandomness can plausibly emerge within simple quantum processes, such as random quantum circuits.

</details>


### [179] [Time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02101)
*Fangqi Dong,Alex Lombardi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机预言机模型下证明了破解量子密码学（破坏量子密钥）的近最优时间-空间下界：$T$ 次查询、$S$ 量子比特非均匀建议的对手从 $n$ 量子比特二进制相位态恢复随机密钥 $k$ 的概率至多为 $O((T^2+\sqrt{ST})/N)$，其中 $N=2^n$。该界优于后量子单向函数已知最优界 $O((T^2+ST)/N)$，展现出量子密码学在抵抗预处理攻击方面的新优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后量子单向函数的时间-空间下界为 $O((T^2+ST)/N)$，在 $S=N$ 时存在平凡攻击，无法在空间超过 $N$ 时提供安全性。本文旨在证明量子密码学（二进制相位态）相比经典密码学在抵抗预处理攻击方面具有新优势，即仅用 $n$ 量子比特通信即可在空间高达 $N^2$ 时保持安全。

Method: 将最优预处理攻击表示为随机矩阵的算子范数，借助迹矩方法（trace-moment method）在对随机预言机的期望下对该范数进行界定。这些迹矩通过压缩预言机（compressed oracles, Zhandry, Crypto 2019）获得自然解释并加以分析，可视为对 Liu（Eurocrypt 2023）方法针对破解后量子密码学时间-空间权衡证明的简化与推广。

Result: 1) 收紧 Liu 对 QROM 中后量子 PRG 的分析，获得区分优势界 $O(T^2/N+\sqrt{ST/N})$；2) 对酉合成（unitary synthesis），将 Lombardi-Ma-Wright（STOC 2024）的单查询下界推广至可进行一次任意函数查询并伴随多项式多次随机预言机自适应查询的对手（函数查询前后均可），并以压缩预言机重新诠释 LMW24 结果；3) 证明随机二进制相位态对空间 $S$ 区分者的伪随机性紧界 $O(\sqrt{S}/N)$。

Conclusion: 本文证明了破解量子密码学的近最优时间-空间下界，揭示了量子密码学在抵抗预处理攻击上优于经典密码学的新优势：$n$ 量子比特通信即可在空间高达 $N^2$ 时保持安全性，而经典情形仅能抵抗空间至多 $N$。方法简洁通用，统一并强化了后量子 PRG、酉合成及二进制相位态伪随机性等多项结果。

Abstract: We prove near-optimal time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography in the random oracle model. Specifically, we show that a $T$-query adversary with $S$ qubits of non-uniform advice can recover a random key $k$ from the $n$-qubit binary phase state $|ψ_k\rangle \propto \sum_{x} R(k,x) |x\rangle$ with probability at most $O(\frac{T^2 + \sqrt{ST}}{N})$ for $N=2^n$. In contrast, the best known bound for post-quantum one-way functions is $O(\frac{T^2 + ST}{N})$, with a trivial attack at $S = N$. This demonstrates a new advantage of quantum cryptography over classical cryptography: $n$ qubits of communication suffice for security against preprocessing attacks with space up to $N^2$ rather than $N$. Our methodology is simple: express the optimal preprocessing attack as the operator norm of a random matrix, and bound this value in expectation over the random oracle via the trace-moment method. These trace moments have a natural interpretation using compressed oracles [Zhandry, Crypto 2019], which we then analyze. This can be viewed as a simplification and generalization of the approach of Liu [Eurocrypt 2023] for proving time-space tradeoffs for breaking post-quantum cryptography. We also prove the following results: (1) We tighten Liu's analysis of post-quantum PRGs in QROM, achieving a distinguishing advantage bound of $O(\frac{T^2}N + \sqrt{\frac{ST}N})$. (2) For unitary synthesis, we extend the one-query lower bound of Lombardi-Ma-Wright [STOC 2024] to hold against adversaries that can make one arbitrary function query along with polynomially many (adaptive) queries to the random oracle, either before or after the function query. This also interprets the original LMW24 result in terms of compressed oracles. (3) Finally, we prove a tight $O(\frac{\sqrt{S}}N)$ bound for the pseudorandomness of random binary phase states against space $S$ distinguishers.

</details>


### [180] [Unitary Schur Sampling of Qudits via Random SWAP Tests: Hunt for Antisymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02103)
*Shrigyan Brahmachari,David Jakab,Henry D. Pfister,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出仅用两体SWAP测试实现通用Schur采样的协议，将其转化为对反对称子系统的搜索，并应用于量子纯度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于电路的Schur采样依赖Clebsch-Gordan变换、广义相位估计或对称群量子傅里叶变换等相干表示论操作，实现复杂。作者希望用更简单的基本操作（两体SWAP测试）实现酉Schur采样，并探索其在量子纯度放大中的应用。

Method: 仅使用两量子比特投影到对称与反对称子空间的成对SWAP测试，反复识别并抽取最大的反对称子系统。该过程被解释为对杨图的随机遍历，在保持SU(d)不可约表示态的同时，在重数子系统上制备一个规范纯态。

Result: 对n个d维量子比特上的任意置换不变混合态，以O(n·min{n,d}^3·log^2(n/ε))次随机SWAP测试达到误差ε的钻石距离。在量子纯度放大应用中，最优纯化态可通过保留单个指定输出量子比特并丢弃其余部分获得。

Conclusion: SWAP测试足以实现酉Schur采样，且具有杨图随机遍历的直观解释；该协议为量子纯度放大提供了简单实现，表明只需保留一个指定输出量子比特即可获得最优纯化态。

Abstract: Schur sampling is a fundamental primitive for extracting permutation-invariant information from many-body quantum systems and has broad applications in quantum information science. Circuit-based implementations typically rely on coherent representation-theoretic operations such as Clebsch-Gordan transforms, generalized phase estimation, or quantum Fourier transforms over the symmetric group. We show that unitary Schur sampling of arbitrary permutation-invariant mixed states on $n$ $d$-dimensional qudits can instead be implemented using only pairwise SWAP tests, namely, two-qudit projective measurements onto the symmetric and antisymmetric subspaces. Our protocol achieves error $ε$ in diamond distance using $O(n\min\{n,d\}^{3}\log^2(n/ε))$ random SWAP tests. The central idea is to repeatedly identify and extract the largest antisymmetric subsystem. This turns unitary Schur sampling into a search for antisymmetry and gives the algorithm a natural interpretation as a stochastic traversal of a Young diagram. The protocol preserves the $\mathrm{SU}(d)$-irrep state while preparing a canonical pure state in the multiplicity subsystem. As an application, we consider quantum purity amplification (QPA), in which multiple noisy copies of a pure quantum state are combined to produce a state of higher purity. We show that the optimal purified state can be obtained by retaining a single designated output qudit and discarding the rest.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum Advantage for Two-Party Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02113)
*Daniel Alabi,Emil T. Khabiboulline*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出信息论量子协议，用于两方汉明距离估计（双方输出相同估计），在Klauck诚实模型下以O(n)通信达到O(1)误差，纯ε-QDP下期望误差至多2/sinh ε+γ，突破了经典信息论协议Ω(√n)或Ω(√n/log n)的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论差分隐私在两方汉明距离估计中，纯DP需Ω(√n)误差，强近似DP需Ω(√n/log n)误差，只有计算假设才能实现O(1)误差。作者试图探究量子通信能否在信息论意义下突破这一精度壁垒，并厘清何种量子资源（非正交消息的保持）才是隐私的来源。

Method: 在Klauck诚实、非抢占、消息保持模型下构造量子协议：核心为受保护的相干往返（guarded coherent round trip）与等Gram刚性（equal-Gram rigidity）原理，阻止诚实方保留与输入相关的互补信息；对近似(ε, δ)-QDP通过精确的hockey-stick散度计算给出更小误差；并区分该模型与较弱的预定义信道隐私及完全保留鲁棒的隐私模型。

Result: 给出O(n)通信的量子协议，满足纯ε量子差分隐私，期望误差至多2/sinh ε+γ（任意γ>0）；对近似(ε, δ)-QDP，精确hockey-stick散度计算得到严格更小的误差，并在δ=o(1/n)时保持O(1)与Ω(√n/log n)的分离。即量子通信可实现O(1)信息论误差，媲美经典仅能在计算假设下达到的精度。

Conclusion: 量子通信能在信息论层面实现常数误差的两方汉明距离估计，显著优于经典信息论协议；非正交量子消息的保持被确认为一种可用的隐私资源，同时该安全性区别于较弱隐私模型，且在完全保留鲁棒模型下测量-中止攻击仍可能成功。

Abstract: We introduce information-theoretically private quantum protocols for two-party Hamming distance when both parties must output the same estimate. Classically, for input length $n$, information-theoretic protocols require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ error under pure differential privacy and $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ error under strong approximate differential privacy, whereas computational security permits $O(1)$ error. In Klauck's honest, nonpreemptive, message-preserving model, we give an $O(n)$-communication quantum protocol with pure $\varepsilon$ quantum differential privacy (QDP) and expected error at most $\frac{2}{\sinh \varepsilon}+γ$, for every $γ>0$. For approximate $(\varepsilon, δ)$ QDP, an exact hockey-stick divergence calculation yields strictly smaller error, while preserving the $O(1)$-versus-$Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ separation for $δ=o(1/n)$. Thus, quantum communication achieves $O(1)$ information-theoretic error, matching the accuracy available classically only under computational assumptions.
  The main construction uses a guarded coherent round trip and an equal-Gram rigidity principle that prevents an honest player from retaining input-dependent complementary information. We separate this model from weaker prescribed-channel privacy, which already admits an exact classical realization, and from fully retention-robust security, against which measurement-and-abort attacks remain possible. Therefore, we identify preservation of non-orthogonal quantum messages as a resource for privacy.

</details>


### [182] [Local random quantum circuits converge to the Porter-Thomas distribution in polynomial depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02125)
*Aniruddha Sen,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明多项式深度砖墙随机电路输出分布以逆多项式速率在全变差距离下收敛到Porter-Thomas分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Porter-Thomas统计是随机量子态与混沌量子多体系统输出分布的特征，在随机电路采样和量子优势实验验证中处于核心地位。低深度随机量子电路的输出统计预期近似Porter-Thomas，但此前缺乏严格的收敛性证明。

Method: 基于近似设计的矩界（moment bounds）、特征函数的解析估计，以及针对逆矩的局域反集中（local anticoncentration）性质，对由最近邻Haar随机门构成的局域随机量子电路固定比特串的输出概率分布进行分析。

Result: 对任意m≥0，深度为O(n^{2m+1}log(n))的电路，其输出分布与Porter-Thomas分布在全变差距离上至多相差O(1/n^m)。

Conclusion: 多项式深度的砖墙随机电路输出分布以逆多项式速率在全变差距离下收敛到Porter-Thomas分布，为随机电路采样及量子优势实验所依赖的Porter-Thomas近似提供了严格的理论依据。

Abstract: Porter-Thomas statistics are a characteristic feature of the output distribution of random quantum states and, more broadly, chaotic quantum many-body systems. Convergence to Porter-Thomas plays a central role in random circuit sampling and experimental demonstrations of quantum advantage, where the output statistics of low-depth random quantum circuits are expected to be approximately Porter-Thomas, despite the absence of a rigorous proof of convergence. We show that the output distribution of polynomial-depth brickwork random circuits converges inverse-polynomially in total variation distance to the Porter-Thomas distribution. Specifically, consider the output probability distribution over a fixed bitstring of a local random quantum circuit, constructed from nearest-neighbor Haar random gates. Then, for any $m \geq 0$, the distribution corresponding to circuits of depth $O(n^{2m+1}\log(n))$ is at most $O(1/n^m)$ far in total variation distance from the Porter-Thomas distribution. Our proof uses moment bounds from approximate designs, analytic estimates for characteristic functions, and a local anticoncentration property for inverse moments.

</details>


### [183] [Spiking neural networks for streaming qubit readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02129)
*Barry M. Dillon,Aqib Javed,Jim Harkin,Patryk Dabkowski,Benjamin Lienhard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出脉冲神经网络（SNN）判别器用于超导量子比特读出，按时间分块流式处理读出信号，实时更新量子态分类分数，精度超越匹配滤波并接近全迹ANN，结合量化感知训练与hls4ml综合可在FPGA上实现低延迟实时读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器的反馈、校准与纠错需要快速且准确的量子比特态判定。在超导平台中，频率复用读出本质上是多变量任务，测量轨迹中包含串扰、弛豫事件及其他瞬态非理想因素，传统匹配滤波难以充分刻画，因此需要更强的判别方法。

Method: 采用脉冲神经网络（SNN）作为读出判别器，将测量窗口划分为连续的时间块（chunk）依次输入网络，利用信号的时间结构并随数据到达不断更新分类分数，而非等待整个读出窗口结束；同时使用量化感知训练（quantisation-aware training）与hls4ml综合工具链，将模型部署到FPGA。

Result: SNN的判别精度优于匹配滤波，并接近使用完整轨迹的人工神经网络（ANN）。此外SNN能够提供随读出信号采集而演化的流式、时间分辨的量子态估计；经量化感知训练与hls4ml综合后，每次FPGA推理更新均可在下一个读出时间块到达前完成。

Conclusion: 脉冲神经网络为FPGA硬件上实现低延迟、实时的量子比特读出提供了有前景的技术路径，并对时间敏感的量子控制与科学推断应用具有更广泛的启示。

Abstract: Fast and accurate qubit-state assignment is essential for feedback, calibration, and error correction in quantum processors. In superconducting platforms, frequency-multiplexed readout makes this task intrinsically multivariate as measured traces can encode crosstalk, qubit-state relaxation events, and other transient nonidealities that are not fully captured by conventional matched filtering. Here, we introduce spiking neural network (SNN) discriminators for superconducting qubit readout. By processing the measurement window in successive time chunks, the networks exploit temporal structure and update classification scores as data arrive, rather than waiting until the end of the readout window. The spiking networks outperform matched-filter discrimination and approach the accuracy of a full-trace artificial neural network. Beyond reaching the performance of artificial neural networks, the key advantage of SNNs is that they provide a streaming, time-resolved estimate of the qubit state that evolves as the readout signal is acquired. Using quantisation-aware training and hls4ml synthesis, we further demonstrate that each FPGA inference update can be completed before the next readout chunk arrives. These results establish spiking neural networks as a promising route to low-latency, real-time qubit readout on FPGA hardware, with broader implications for time-critical quantum-control and scientific-inference applications.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum impurity models: easy at equilibrium, universal in motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02130)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sergey Bravyi,Anirban Chowdhury,Arkopal Dutt,Alexandru Gheorghiu,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子杂质模型的平衡性质（基态能量、热平衡）可被经典多项式时间算法高效逼近，而时间演化模拟是BQP完全的，构成平衡与非平衡计算复杂性的鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型描述嵌入自由费米子大浴中的小相互作用子系统，是凝聚态与量子场论中的重要模型。作者旨在厘清其基态能量、热平衡态与时间演化三类问题的计算复杂性界限，并改进已知经典算法的效率。

Method: 针对含n个费米模、杂质尺寸为常数的哈密顿量H：(1) 基态能量经典逼近；(2) 逆温度β下的亥姆霍兹自由能与热场双态经典描述计算；(3) 时间演化模拟的复杂性归类。核心技巧是利用按能标与Krylov深度组织的基底下，多粒子浴激发的指数压制特性；并构造一个固定量子处理器，其程序以自由传播费米子流的形式输入，实现普适量子计算。

Result: (1) 基态能量可达加性误差ε，经典算法运行时间为poly(n,1/ε)，优于此前最优准多项式时间；(2) 自由能与热场双态经典描述可在poly(n,β,1/ε)时间内以精度ε计算；(3) 时间演化e^{-iHt}的模拟为BQP完全，即使H时间无关且杂质尺寸固定为常数。

Conclusion: 量子杂质模型呈现平衡性质经典可高效逼近、而动力学演化具备普适量子计算能力的二分性。所用能标-Krylov深度基下的指数压制分析为杂质模型的经典可解性提供了统一框架，结果对量子模拟优势的来源给出了明确界定。

Abstract: A quantum impurity model describes a small interacting subsystem embedded into a large bath of free fermions. Here we study the computational complexity of calculating the ground energy, thermal equilibrium, and dynamical properties of these models. Our work reveals a sharp contrast: equilibrium properties can be efficiently approximated by classical means, whereas time evolution can implement a universal quantum computation. More precisely, let $H$ be the Hamiltonian of an impurity model with $n$ fermionic modes and a constant-size impurity. We show that: (1) the ground energy of $H$ can be approximated to additive error $\varepsilon$ by a classical algorithm with runtime $\textsf{poly}(n,1/\varepsilon)$, improving on the quasi-polynomial runtime of the best previously known algorithm; (2) at inverse temperature $β$, the Helmholtz free energy and a classical description of the thermofield double state can be computed to precision $\varepsilon$ in time $\textsf{poly}(n,β,1/\varepsilon)$; (3) simulating the time evolution $e^{-iHt}$ is $\textsf{BQP}$-complete, for $H$ that is time-independent and has a fixed, constant impurity size. Our algorithms exploit exponential suppression of multi-particle bath excitations in a basis organized by energy scale and Krylov depth. Our universality construction realizes a stationary quantum processor whose program arrives in a stream of freely propagating fermions.

</details>


### [185] [Optimal transducers using symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02133)
*Benoît Dubus,Julien Ladeuze,Jérémie Roland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用状态转换问题的对称群，简化了最优量子换能器（transducer）的构造，并导出了若干基本量子算法的常数为最优的换能器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换能器框架可将（对偶）敌手半正定规划的可行点直接转化为量子算法，且其最优换能复杂度等于敌手界（即 Las Vegas 复杂度），并具有精确复合、无控制误差开销的优势。但构造高效甚至最优换能器仍需解敌手 SDP 并显式构造酉算子，难度较大，因此需要简化这一过程。

Method: 作者引入状态转换问题的对称群：(1) 通过对称化论证，证明最优催化剂总可取为在对称群某一表示下协变；(2) 证明换能器纠缠（intertwine）群的两个不同表示，因而可在 Hilbert 空间同型分解中取分块对角形式。随后将表示论方法应用于构造最优算法。

Result: 利用上述方法，作者为多种广泛使用的量子算法原语导出了最优换能器及其最优常数，包括无结构搜索、振幅放大与振幅估计。该方法将此前将表示论用于计算敌手下界的工作（Høyer–Lee–Špalek 2007；Ambainis–Magnin–Roetteler–Roland 2011）推广到了最优算法的系统性构造。

Conclusion: 对称性可同时简化换能器的两步构造——催化剂对称化与换能器的分块对角化——从而使最优换能器及最优常数的系统推导成为可能，把表示论在敌手下界计算中的用途扩展为最优量子算法的构造工具。

Abstract: Transducers (Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu, 2024) are a quantum computing framework describing a quantum algorithm as a unitary converting an input state into a target state using a catalyst, an auxiliary vector that is left unchanged. They are a powerful tool in quantum algorithm design, especially in the context of quantum query complexity: feasible points of the (dual) adversary semidefinite program directly translate into transducers and the optimal transduction complexity is equal to the adversary bound, i.e. the Las Vegas complexity, which is known to characterize bounded-error quantum query complexity. Moreover, contrary to bounded-error algorithms, transducers compose exactly, which limits overheads due to controlling errors in algorithms constructed by composition. Constructing efficient, let alone optimal, transducers in terms of quantum query complexity nevertheless remains a hard task since it still requires solving the adversary SDP and constructing the unitary to obtain an explicit algorithm. In this paper, we show how using the symmetry group of state-conversion problems simplifies both steps. First, using a symmetrization argument, we prove an optimal catalyst can always be chosen covariant under a representation of the symmetry group. Second, we prove that the transducer intertwines two different representations of the group and can thus be chosen block diagonal in the isotypic decomposition of the Hilbert space. Using those methods, we then derive optimal transducers, with optimal constants, for different widely used quantum algorithmic primitives, such as unstructured search, amplitude amplification and amplitude estimation. Our approach extends previous work on the use of representation theory to compute adversary lower bounds (Høyer, Lee, and {\v S}palek, 2007; Ambainis, Magnin, Roetteler and Roland, 2011) to the systematic construction of optimal algorithms.

</details>


### [186] [A Generalized quantum Stein lemma on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02134)
*Li Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意von Neumann代数上、针对凸且张量稳定族的i.i.d.正态态的广义量子Stein引理：在存在与零假设有限相对熵的备择态时，任意第一类错误容限下最坏情况第二类错误指数由正则化相对熵给出且具有强逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Stein引理刻画假设检验中第二类错误的最优指数衰减速率。已有结果多限于特定代数或特定态族，本文旨在将Stein引理推广至任意von Neumann代数上的凸、张量稳定族，突破原有适用范围。

Method: 结合相对熵的hockey-stick散度积分表示、模检验界（modular testing bound）以及凸极小极大论证，在i.i.d.正态态与凸、张量稳定备择族假设下构造并分析最优检验。

Result: 证明了在任意第一类错误容限ε∈(0,1)下，最坏情况第二类错误指数等于正则化相对熵，并建立了强逆性质。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子Stein引理推广到任意von Neumann代数上的凸、张量稳定族，给出最坏情况第二类错误指数与强逆的统一刻画。

Abstract: We prove a generalized quantum Stein lemma for i.i.d. normal states against convex, tensor-stable families on arbitrary von Neumann algebras. Assuming the existence of an alternative state with finite relative entropy from the null state, we show that, at every type-I error tolerance $\varepsilon\in (0,1)$, the worst case type-II error exponent is achieved with the regularized relative entropy with a strong converse. The proof combines the integral representation of relative entropy by hockey-stick divergences, a modular testing bound, and convex minimax argument.

</details>


### [187] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为强ε-近似酉k-设计给出了单个输出概率分布的定量保证：每个输出概率分布与Porter-Thomas分布在Kolmogorov距离下误差为O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε)，该界在常数因子内最优；若设计具有局部平移不变性，则可获得关于k指数级改进的误差界，并推广至全变差距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路旨在高效复现理想随机量子演化的统计性质，近似酉设计通过矩匹配逼近Haar随机酉算子。然而，现有研究缺乏关于这些设计能否准确复现单个输出概率完整分布的定量保证。本文旨在建立此类保证，并揭示矩匹配所能达到的分布精度极限以及提升精度所需的结构条件。

Method: 作者对n量子比特上的任意强ε-近似酉k-设计进行理论分析，考察其每个单个输出概率的分布与有限维Porter-Thomas分布（参数为1和2^n-1的beta分布）之间的Kolmogorov距离。通过证明匹配下界说明常数因子最优性；进一步引入局部不变性（在log k+O(1)量子比特上的酉平移下不变），分析Kolmogorov距离与全变差距离下的误差，并将其应用于输出分布Shannon熵的估计。

Result: 对于任意强ε-近似酉k-设计，每个输出概率分布与Porter-Thomas分布的Kolmogorov距离为O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε)，该界在常数因子内最优。若设计在作用于log k+O(1)量子比特的酉平移下不变，则Kolmogorov距离误差降至2^{-Ω(k)}+O(ε)，全变差距离误差为2^{-Ω(k)}+O(ε log(2/ε))，即关于k获得指数级改进。此外，若强近似设计每个输出概率的边缘分布与Haar对应分布在Kolmogorov距离η内，则输出分布的期望Shannon熵与Haar值之差为O(√η)。

Conclusion: 本文精确刻画了矩匹配所保证的分布精度，并指出常数因子最优的均匀界需要额外结构。局部不变性可显著（指数级）提升精度，并在更强的全变差度量下给出保证。这些结果明确了随机量子电路近似设计的分布准确性界限及其改进途径，并可用于Shannon熵等应用。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [188] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 任意单次纠错码都是自纠错的；任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码可变换为[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码，具备单次量子纠错与O(log m)并行时间的局部解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造同时具备单次纠错能力、高效局部解码器、并可提升误差抑制能力的量子LDPC码，是该领域的核心目标。

Method: 先给出任意single-shot码自纠错的简洁证明；再将[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码扩展为[[nm,k]]码，配合运行时间O(log m)的并行局部解码器，在联合局部随机物理与测量噪声下分析阈值与误差抑制。

Result: 在阈值p_RG>0以下，T轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率上界为O(Tn)exp[-Ω(m^α)]；若输入码原有阈值p_c与误差抑制Ω(n^β)，输出码将误差抑制增强至Ω(m^α n^β)，阈值保持为p_RG，且当p_c<p_RG时实现增强。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了将一般CSS QLDPC码升级为单次纠错码的通用框架，兼具高阈值、强误差抑制与高效并行解码，并可对已有单次纠错码进一步放大其误差抑制能力。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [189] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在预言机模型下证明解码量子干涉（DQI）相对所有多项式时间经典算法具有严格优势，并提出改进算法进一步扩大该分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI作为求解近似优化问题的新范式，性能保证强，但其能否真正可证明地超越所有多项式时间经典算法尚属核心未解问题。

Method: 构造折叠多项式交集（folded OPI）优化任务，接受集随机选取并通过成员资格预言机访问；基于Jordan等的DQI框架，将Yamakawa与Zhandry精确搜索预言机分离的经典下界方法推广至近似情形；借鉴Sun与Wootters、Horinaga与Yamakawa、Jo的最新进展，设计改进版DQI算法。

Result: 在近似比上建立多项式时间经典算法与DQI算法间的严格差距；改进算法在folded OPI上给出更大的严格差距。码率0.3时，DQI与改进算法期望得分约为0.85与0.95，而经典算法欲以常数概率超过0.65阈值需超多项式次成员资格查询。

Conclusion: DQI在预言机设定下可证明地优于所有多项式时间经典算法，且改进算法带来更强的量子分离，为量子近似优化优势提供了新的理论证据。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [190] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 给出了一个确定性经典算法，在poly(n,1/ε)时间内将常深度量子电路输出概率|⟨x|U|0^n⟩|²估计到加性误差ε，显著优于此前n^{O(log n)}、几何局域下n^{O(log log n)}及2D局域n^{O(1)}的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计常深度量子电路特定输出比特串的概率是量子计算与经典模拟之间的核心问题。此前最优经典算法需n^{O(log n)}时间，几何局域情形亦需n^{O(log log n)}或2D下n^{O(1)}，存在明显的时间复杂度瓶颈，亟需更高效的经典模拟方法。

Method: 提出一种确定性经典算法，针对由有界扇入门、任意连接构成的常深度量子电路U，估计输出概率|⟨x|U|0^n⟩|²，在poly(n,1/ε)时间内达到加性误差ε。

Result: 算法在poly(n,1/ε)时间内完成估计，突破此前n^{O(log n)}（一般情形）、n^{O(log log n)}（几何局域）以及n^{O(1)}（2D几何局域电路）的时间复杂度上界，实现了多项式时间的经典模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作将常深度量子电路输出概率的经典估计复杂度降至多项式时间，统一并大幅改进了各类连接与几何约束下的已有结果，对理解常深度量子电路的经典可模拟性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [191] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了 QAC⁰ 在误差容忍与门集限制下的鲁棒性：一，利用多副本情形下的精确振幅放大消除并行 W-test 的固有误差，使 QAC⁰ 能精确模拟 TC⁰，且对任意固定素数 p，精确 QAC⁰/EQAC⁰ 可计算 AC⁰[p] 之外的全布尔函数；二，证明 QAC⁰ 可用广义 Toffoli、S 和 Hadamard 门近似实现，且该近似电路可经典高效构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 QAC⁰ 结果通常允许非零误差，且使用任意单量子比特门。作者希望厘清两个问题：计算布尔函数时允许的误差是否必要；QAC⁰ 的计算能力是否依赖于任意单比特门可用这一条件。

Method: 第一，对 grier_morris_wu 的并行 W-test 应用多副本框架下的精确振幅放大，以消除其固有误差。第二，构造门集受限的近似模拟，仅使用广义 Toffoli、S 与 Hadamard 门，并给出从原电路经典描述高效构造近似电路的方法。

Result: 可完全消除并行 W-test 的误差；QAC⁰ 能以多项式副本精确模拟 TC⁰；对每个固定素数 p，精确 QAC⁰（EQAC⁰）可计算不在 AC⁰[p] 中的全布尔函数；任意 QAC⁰ 电路可由仅含广义 Toffoli、S、Hadamard 的 QAC⁰ 电路近似实现，且可经典高效构造。

Conclusion: QAC⁰ 对误差容忍和门集限制都具有鲁棒性：误差可被精确消除，门集可大幅受限而保持近似计算能力，并且近似电路可高效生成。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [192] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：借助快速单比特控制与连通的参考骨干，仅用一个可测量子比特即可在海森堡极限下学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数，并给出链上总查询时间Õ(N)的并行学习架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议通常假设能够对整个系统进行直接测量，即需要访问全部量子比特，这在大规模多体系统中难以实现。因此需要研究在仅有局部测量接口（甚至单点测量）条件下能否高效、高精度地学习大量哈密顿量参数。

Method: 利用快速单比特控制与连通的参考骨干，通过量子信号处理（quantum signal processing）合成的鲁棒SWAP门实现相干态转移，将远处哈密顿量参数下的演化态传递至唯一可测量子比特，且无需对中间链路的哈密顿量参数进行预先标定；进一步提出并行学习架构以加速参数估计。

Result: 单可测量子比特即可学习有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数，精度达到海森堡极限；在N量子比特链上，并行架构总查询时间为Õ(N)，并保持海森堡极限精度；这些标度与精度、信息传播下界在忽略对数因子意义下相符，方法可推广到任意有界度相互作用图。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了仅通过局部测量接口学习大量多体哈密顿量参数的可扩展路径，突破了传统协议对全系统直接测量访问的依赖。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于有效剖面复杂度的稠密量子p-spin模型态制备复杂度方法，突破光锥限制，导出近基态能量所需Ω(n²/logn)门数等多项下界与深度-宽度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电路光锥界仅反映局域性，无法刻画稠密p-spin哈密顿量态制备的复杂度；需引入超越光锥的工具以给出更强的门数、深度与纠缠下界。

Method: 定义由Pauli剖面度量熵导出的有效剖面复杂度：剖面记录恰好支撑在p个量子比特上的Pauli期望。结合Berta等非对称量子de Finetti定理的改编与高斯过程熵界进行证明；并应用于Parham魔幻层级的两个方向，分析首层反向魔幻中浅电路后接无限制Clifford电路的情形。

Result: 二次有效剖面复杂度一致有界的类与基态能量保持√n的正间隔；次二次复杂度类在领头阶无法超越乘积态基准。达到近基态能量需Ω(n²/logn)个单、双比特门（任意可丢弃辅助比特）。得到深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与矩阵积态键维下界，以及在总电路宽度O(n)下各固定Parham魔幻层级的两个方向的阻碍；首层反向魔幻允许任意多干净辅助比特。Clifford+T电路若T门为o(n)，即使有无限制Clifford操作与任意可丢弃辅助比特，也无领头阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效剖面复杂度为稠密量子多体态制备提供了超越光锥的严格下界框架，统一涵盖门数、深度、纠缠与魔幻资源限制。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [194] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了量子杂质模型的计算复杂性，证明其静态性质可在经典计算机上多项式时间高效模拟，而动力学性质（如非平衡格林函数）在经典计算机上困难，却能在量子计算机上高效模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型既是相互作用量子物质的典型模型，也是现代电子结构方法中的关键计算原语。研究者希望厘清该类问题在经典与量子可计算性之间的边界，明确哪些性质可经典高效计算、哪些需要量子计算才能获得优势。

Method: 作者对量子杂质模型（小的相互作用费米子子系统耦合到大的非相互作用浴）的模拟问题进行了全面的计算复杂性分析，分别针对静态性质（基态能量、配分函数）给出经典算法并分析其时间复杂度，针对动力学性质（非平衡格林函数）分析其经典与量子计算复杂性。

Result: 静态性质方面：(1) 以加性精度 δ 估计基态能量可在 poly(n,δ⁻¹) 时间内完成；(2) 以相对精度 δ 估计逆温度 β 下的配分函数可在 poly(n,β,δ⁻¹) 时间内完成，其中 n 为系统规模。这将基态能量估计的已知最优复杂度从拟多项式改进为多项式，并首次为热平衡下模拟杂质模型建立了严格的多项式时间保证。动力学方面：非平衡格林函数的计算即使在有限温度下也具有完整量子计算能力，经典计算困难。

Conclusion: 该研究排除了静态性质计算上超多项式量子加速的可能性，但指出在模拟非平衡杂质物理方面存在实现量子优势的途径，从而在经典与量子可计算性之间划出了清晰的边界。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [195] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色与费米系统中高斯分解秩的下界问题，证明了任意纯非高斯态张量幂的近似边界高斯秩随模数呈指数增长，为基于分解的经典模拟范式建立了根本性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相干分解为经典可处理自由态的模拟范式中，分解秩决定模拟复杂度，证明该数的强下界（如量子比特稳定子秩问题）极为困难且数学内涵丰富。作者希望在高斯版本的分解秩问题上建立类似的鲁棒指数下界，以刻画玻色与费米系统中经典模拟的能力与极限。

Method: 证明结合了两条技术路线：费米子情形通过将问题归约到四模并利用马约拉纳谱界；玻色子情形采用高斯后选择方法并配合基于熵的秩界。对四模费米GHZ态和玻色单光子态分别给出显式指数下界。

Result: 对有限模上任意纯非高斯态（费米情形附加确定宇称条件），其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩在任何低于1的固定范数误差下至少指数增长。玻色结果无需对目标态能量作任何假设，四模费米GHZ态与玻色单光子态均得到显式指数下界。

Conclusion: 非高斯性普遍意味着指数级的高斯分解复杂度，这为基于分解的玻色与费米系统经典模拟设定了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [196] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究置换不变性对量子信道优化优势的限制，证明优化相干信息每use收敛于单次使用最大值，并给出相应的强逆定理、完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量的统一变分框架，以及输出熵每use不能低于单次最小值的熵极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的关联可提升量子通信速率，使得量子容量的确定需要对任意大块进行优化，这构成核心困难。作者希望借助置换不变性来限制这种关联带来的优势，并统一理解若干信道量。

Method: 采用全置换不变性、De Finetti约化与置换协方差方法，在统一的变分框架下分析优化相干信息、纯源纠缠生成的强逆、完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量，以及输出熵。

Result: 优化后的每use相干信息收敛到单次使用最大值，并在有限块长达到其上确界；有限块超可加性仍可能存在，而渐近优化退化为单次信道使用。对置换不变输入的纯源纠缠生成建立强逆：任何高于单次相干信息最大值的固定速率下保真度趋于零。同时揭示完全有界范数与sandwiched Rényi信道量的共同结构，并给出其乘性与可加性结果的系统替代证明；对置换不变输入，输出熵每use不能渐近低于单次最小值。

Conclusion: 置换不变性将通信量的渐近优化归结为单次信道使用，同时推广到纠缠生成强逆、范数与Rényi量的乘性/可加性以及输出熵控制，体现出对称性在量子通信界限与熵控制中的统一作用。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [197] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从Gibbs态副本中学习稠密SYK哈密顿量的问题，突破了几何局域性或有限相互作用度的限制，利用随机平均场结构实现了多项式样本与准多项式时间的学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习算法通常依赖几何局域性或有限的相互作用度，而SYK模型中每个四次相互作用与Θ(n^3)个其他项重叠，导致这些算法不适用。因此需要新的方法来克服这一障碍。

Method: 利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，从Gibbs态副本中学习哈密顿量。证明在任意常温度下，以高概率（对SYK耦合）可以反多项式精度学习整个哈密顿量；当逆温度限制为足够小的常数时，构造准多项式时间学习算法。

Result: 在任意常温度下，以高概率（对SYK耦合）使用多项式数量的样本即可将整个哈密顿量学习到反多项式精度；在逆温度为足够小的常数时，给出了准多项式时间学习算法，该算法在性质上不同于样本高效算法。

Conclusion: 通过利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，成功克服了稠密相互作用带来的学习障碍，实现了样本高效和准多项式时间的哈密顿量学习，为稠密量子系统学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [198] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究被全局酉算符遮蔽的哈密顿量学习问题，证明该问题DQC1-hard，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复准粒子谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模中，均值场理论、格林函数和密度泛函理论等经典方法需要准粒子谱信息。然而当哈密顿量的非相互作用结构被未知全局酉算符U遮蔽时，如何在不重构U的前提下恢复能量权重E_i成为一个具有实际意义的问题。

Method: 问题设定为学习H=U†(∑_{i∈I}E_i n_i + H_O)U中非相互作用模式的权重E_i。作者首先证明该问题DQC1-hard。随后提出SPICES（single-particle inference via contour estimators），将Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理结合，利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点对应的傅里叶频率之间的关系，在自然假设下以Wasserstein距离高效恢复谱分布。

Result: 该学习问题被证明为DQC1-hard，说明即使存在非相互作用结构，问题仍具有本质量子困难性。SPICES在特定自然假设下能够高效恢复谱分布，并以Wasserstein距离作为精度度量。

Conclusion: 由于准粒子谱在下游材料计算中普遍存在，且SPICES简单实用，作者认为SPICES是通往材料模拟中量子应用的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Exact Material Circulation Surface in an Unsteady Viscous Vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00082)
*Khalid M. Saqr*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文为含源非定常 Kiknadze–Krasnov 涡旋推导出一个精确的物质输运面：在归一化半径 x=βr² 下满足 Dx/Dt=4νβ[(s-1)-x]，故 x=s-1 为物质圆柱面，同时是最大涡量位置与零黏性环量传递边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为含源非定常涡旋寻找一个同时具备拉格朗日物质面、峰值涡量轨迹与环量传输零边界三重身份的精确曲面，并以此作为物质面追踪与环量输运数值方法的基准解。

Method: 从不可压缩 Navier–Stokes 方程出发，对含源非定常 Kiknadze–Krasnov 涡旋的单分布模态（s>1）作相似变换，令标度半径 x=βr²，推导其物质导数方程；进一步求解完整速度场、压力场、参数许可范围与多模态限制。

Result: 标度半径满足 Dx/Dt=4νβ[(s-1)-x]，故 x=s-1 在任意允许的时变应变下均为物质圆柱面；该面同时是分布涡量的最大值所在处，其封闭环量 ΓP(s,s-1) 恒定，因为黏性环量传递项 2πνr ∂_r ω_z 在该面上恒为零。已知的稳态含源-应变驻点圆柱作为极限情形被恢复。

Conclusion: 该运动曲面同时给出拉格朗日径向分割、峰值涡量轨迹与零传递环量边界，可作为物质面追踪与给定应变下环量输运数值误差评估的精确基准。

Abstract: An exact material circulation surface is derived for the source-bearing unsteady Kiknadze--Krasnov vortex. For a single distributed mode with $s>1$, the scaled radius $x=βr^2$ obeys $\frac{\mathrm D x}{\mathrm D t}=4νβ\big[(s-1)-x\big],$ so $x=s-1$ defines a material cylinder for any admissible time-dependent strain. The same cylinder contains the maximum distributed vorticity. Its enclosed circulation, $ΓP(s,s-1)$, is constant because the viscous circulation transfer $2πνr\,\partial_rω_z$ vanishes identically there. Thus one moving surface simultaneously provides a Lagrangian radial partition, a peak-vorticity locus, and a zero-transfer circulation boundary. The known steady source--strain stagnation cylinder is recovered as a limiting case. The full velocity and pressure fields, admissible parameter ranges, multimode restrictions, and radial-scale dynamics are also derived and verified directly from the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. The surface can be used as an exact benchmark for assessing numerical errors in material-surface tracking and circulation transport under prescribed strain.

</details>


### [200] [Fluid drags on a sphere at finite Reynolds number--a classical problem examined afresh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00334)
*James Q Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文评估了Klyachko(1934)提出的双参数分数形式阻力系数公式Cd(Re)的精度，验证其在Re<1000及扩展至Re<3×10⁵范围内的适用性，并展示了该公式在气溶胶球体直线运动研究中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球体在有限雷诺数下的阻力系数Cd缺乏闭式数学解，现有计算公式均依赖对实验数据的曲线拟合，导致公式繁多、拟合参数不断增加。作者希望寻找一种形式简洁、参数少且能实现运动方程闭式积分的公式。

Method: 以Klyachko(1934)的双分数参数公式为对象，利用近期发展的公式计算均方根相对误差(RMS relative error)进行精度评估，并将其作为分段连续函数扩展至Re<3×10⁵，提出轻微修正以提升精度，最后通过直线加速、终端速度计算等示例加以演示。

Result: 均方根相对误差计算表明，Klyachko公式在Re<1000范围内精度合理，经扩展后可作为分段连续函数适用于Re<3×10⁵，且通过轻微修正可获得显著精度提升。

Conclusion: Klyachko公式以仅两个分数参数实现了良好精度并支持运动方程的闭式积分，为气溶胶球体直线运动（如加速过程、终端速度计算）等经典问题提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: Evaluating the drag coefficient Cd for a sphere moving in a fluid at finite Reynolds number Re has become a classical research subject in fluid dynamics, with publications appearing continuously by numerous authors over the years due to the unavailability of closed-form mathematical solutions. To date, mathematical formulas for calculating Cd as a function of Re can only be obtained by correlation curve fitting to experimental data. The consequence is the coexistence of various formulas with different mathematical fitting functions and an increasing number of fitting parameters. The present study focuses on examining the accuracy of Klyachko's 1934 formula for Cd with two common fractions as fitting parameters, which could enable closed form integration of a simplified equation governing rectilinear motion of aerosol spheres. The computed values of root-mean-square relative error from recently developed formulas show reasonable accuracy of the Klyachko formula for Re less than 1000 and its extension to Re less than 3e5 as a piecewise continuous function, with the possibility of significant improvement through a slight modification. Hence, a few demonstrative examples are presented using the extended Klyachko formula in the study of rectilinear acceleration, the calculation of the terminal velocity of aerosol spheres, and so forth, to shed fresh light on how this classical problem can be studied.

</details>


### [201] [On the spectra of strong turbulence on water surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00777)
*Gregory Falkovich*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文回顾了水面重力波直接级联谱理论的发展，指出弱湍流理论适用于长波，而短波区由波浪破碎主导；作者给出了破碎波频率谱的幅值估计，并论证该频率谱在整个级联过程中保持不变，同时说明由弱湍流向强湍流的转变源于谱非局域相互作用，且发生尺度比量纲估计更早。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清重力波直接级联从弱湍流到强湍流（破碎）区间谱理论的争议，纠正Phillips关于破碎波波数谱幅值与频率谱形式的错误，并给出更合理的谱预测。

Method: 基于Zakharov弱湍流理论处理长波，结合波浪破碎（表面尖点）分析短波强湍流区，采用谱非局域相互作用分析弱-强湍流转变尺度和破碎波频率谱幅值。

Result: 长波可用弱湍流理论描述；破碎波频率谱斜率形式由Kuznetsov给出，本文估计其幅值并认为该频率谱贯穿整个级联不变；弱湍流至强湍流的转变由谱非局域相互作用引起，发生尺度大于量纲估计；强湍流谱幅值不具普适性，依赖弱湍流级联的泵浦；非局域性可能源于短波破碎受其所处最长波幅值决定。

Conclusion: 重力波直接级联谱需区分弱湍流与强湍流（破碎）区间；频率谱在破碎主导下保持稳定形式，强湍流谱幅值受泵浦影响而非普适，谱非局域性是理解弱-强湍流转变的关键。

Abstract: Here, I give a brief history of the theory, with twists and turns, and list possible predictions for the spectra of the direct cascade of gravity waves on the water. The description of long waves is straightforward within the weak-turbulence theory developed by Zakharov and his school. As the energy cascade proceeds to shorter scales, the mean surface steepness grows, and wave-breaking events appear with increasing frequency. Considering surface cusps, Phillips suggested a wavenumber spectrum (with a wrong magnitude, as I argue here) and a frequency spectrum whose whole form is incorrect. The right form for the frequency spectral slope due to breaking waves was suggested by Kuznetsov. Here we estimate the magnitude and argue that the frequency spectrum is expected to continue unchanged through the whole cascade. The transition from weak to strong turbulence in the wavenumber and spatio-temporal spectra is due to spectrally nonlocal interactions and happens earlier (at larger scales) than the dimensional estimate suggests. As a result, the magnitude of the strong-turbulence spectrum is not universal but depends on the pumping for a sufficiently long weak-turbulence cascade. The reason for nonlocality is likely that breaking of shorter waves is determined by the magnitude of the longest wave on whose crest they are running.

</details>


### [202] [Scalar mixing by stagnation points on solid surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00790)
*Gaute Linga,Tanguy Le Borgne,Joris Heyman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文建立了多孔介质中薄溶质条带/片在与固体边界相连的分离线（上游驻点）附近的标量混合理论，得到混合时间尺度 \(t_B\sim Pe^{1/3}\) 及浓度衰减与宽度增长的分段标度律，并经数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有将多孔介质混合与无穷小流体元变形相联系的拉格朗日框架，通常忽略或简化了流体与固体表面的相互作用；本文旨在刻画固体边界对溶质混合的影响。

Method: 考虑薄溶质斑块（条带或薄片）在单一通用障碍物附近的输运，特别是沿连接固体边界驻点的分离线，利用流动与扩散耦合在此情形下可解析处理的特点，构建上游驻点附近标量混合的完整理论，并用数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 识别出混合时间尺度 \(t_B\sim Pe^{1/3}\)（\(Pe\) 为佩克莱数），用于判定标量衰减由流体拉伸还是由固体边界相互作用主导。渐近地，当 \(t<t_B\) 时，最大浓度 \(c_{\rm max}\) 按 \(t^{-5/2}\) 衰减，宽度 \(\sigma\) 按 \(t^{1/2}\) 增长；当 \(t>t_B\) 时，\(c_{\rm max}\) 呈指数衰减，宽度 \(\sigma\sim s_B\sim Pe^{-1/3}\) 趋于常数，其中 \(s_B\) 为区别于剪切流中出现的混合长度尺度。这些标度及对 \(Pe\) 的依赖对无滑移驻点具有普适性，且与开放流中的驻点定性不同。

Conclusion: 该理论完整刻画了无滑移固体边界上游驻点附近的标量混合，揭示了由 \(Pe^{1/3}\) 控制的混合时间尺度及分段标度行为，并表明其普适性及与开放流驻点情形的本质差异。

Abstract: Recent advances have linked mixing in porous media to the deformation of infinitesimal fluid elements. However, these Lagrangian frameworks generally neglect or simplify interactions with solid surfaces. We consider transport of thin solute blobs (strips or sheets) near single generic obstacles, in particular along separation lines (separatrices) which connect to stagnation points at solid boundaries. In this case, we demonstrate that the coupling between flow and diffusion is analytically tractable. We provide a complete theory for scalar mixing near upstream stagnation points, which we validate by numerical simulations. We identify a mixing time scale $t_B \sim Pe^{1/3}$, where $Pe$ is the Péclet number, which controls whether scalar decay is driven by fluid stretching or by interaction with the solid boundary. Asymptotically, for times $t < t_B$, we find that the maximum concentration $c_{\rm max}$ decays as $t^{-5/2}$ and that the width $σ$ grows like $t^{1/2}$. For $t > t_B$, we find exponential decay of $c_{\rm max}$ and constant width $σ\sim s_B \sim Pe^{-1/3}$, where $s_B$ is a mixing length scale distinct from that which appears in shear flows. The asymptotic scalings of $c_{\rm max}(t)$ and $σ(t)$, and their dependence on $Pe$, are universal to no-slip stagnation points and qualitatively different from stagnation points in open flows.

</details>


### [203] [A conservative micro-continuum-cellular automaton method for multispecies biofilm dynamics in complex flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01565)
*Soyoung Kim,Yinuo Noah Yao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出守恒型微观连续体-元胞自动机方法模拟复杂流动中的多物种生物膜动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂流动与几何中多物种生物膜的模拟需要兼顾流动、反应输运、悬浮菌与生物膜界面演化，并保证生物量守恒与物种组成一致，尤其涉及非均匀网格与部分占据单元。

Method: 耦合Darcy-Brinkman-Stokes方程、反应输运、悬浮细菌与生物膜动力学；采用两阶段元胞自动机算法处理生物膜再分布与界面演化；将生物膜视为演化多孔介质，在部分占据单元间守恒再分布多物种生物量，显式考虑供体与受体单元体积以守恒生物量并保持物种组成（适用于非均匀网格）。

Result: 在扩散主导基准算例（单物种指进、多物种分层）中验证；对矩形凸起上的流动与生长进行网格收敛性研究；应用于膜曝气生物膜反应器中的反向扩散生物膜。结果复现了预期生物膜形态与分层。

Conclusion: 该框架能捕捉耦合流动、底物输运与生物膜动力学的形态与分层，为复杂流动与几何中的多物种生物膜模拟提供灵活计算方法。

Abstract: We develop a conservative micro-continuum-cellular automaton method for simulating multispecies biofilm dynamics in complex flows. The proposed method couples the Darcy-Brinkman-Stokes equations, reactive transport, suspended bacteria, and biofilm dynamics with a two-stage cellular automaton algorithm for biofilm redistribution and interface evolution. While treating biofilms as evolving porous media, we ensure conservative redistribution of multispecies biomass across partially occupied cells. Donor and recipient cell volumes are explicitly accounted for to conserve biomass and preserve species composition on non-uniform meshes. The proposed method is assessed against diffusion-dominated benchmark cases, including single-species fingering and multispecies stratification, and is further evaluated through a mesh-convergence study for flow and growth over a rectangular bump. The framework is then applied to counter-diffusional biofilms in a membrane-aerated biofilm reactor as a canonical example. The results demonstrate that the framework captures the expected biofilm morphology and stratification in systems involving coupled flow, substrate transport, and biofilm dynamics. The proposed method provides a flexible computational approach for simulating multispecies biofilm dynamics in complex flows and geometries.

</details>


### [204] [Flow separation and turbulence production over trapezoidal protrusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01570)
*Shahar Zuckerman,Jibu Tom Jose,Ori Levi,Omri Ram*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过立体粒子图像测速实验，研究12个梯形凸起物（L/h=1–4，后倾角30°、45°、90°）周围的湍流特性，揭示长度主导流场拓扑转变、后倾角主要影响近壁湍动能，并借助面内偏斜生产分解阐明局部湍流生成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸起物后表面倾角对湍流的影响取决于接近后缘的分离流状态，但该依赖关系尚不明确；研究旨在厘清凸起上表面长度与后倾角如何共同决定尾缘分离流结构及湍流生成。

Method: 在基于高度的雷诺数约5×10^4、来流边界层厚度与凸起高度相当的条件下，对12个梯形凸起（上表面长高比L/h=1、2、3、4，后表面角30°、45°、90°）进行立体粒子图像测速（SPIV）测量，并对面内偏斜生产项进行分解，将雷诺应力与平均应变幅值的影响同主�lAxis相对取向的影响分离。

Result: L/h=1和2时，连通的反向流动区越过上表面延伸至尾流；L/h=3和4时，上表面再附将两区域分离。长度主导拓扑变化及共同空间位置处的湍流水平差异，后倾角影响在近壁面最强。自L/h=2起，较缓面近壁湍动能更高，垂直面湍动能较低但展向占比更大。对倾斜面，面内偏斜生产的贡献在L/h=1到2之间由负转正，发生在上表面再附之前；随后随长度增加主要由张量幅值增大驱动，最大贡献出现在L/h=4、30°工况，强生产区保持贴近后表面。

Conclusion: 局部湍流生产共同取决于接近后缘的流态与高能剪切层相对于后表面的位置；上表面长度控制流场拓扑与生产符号转变，后倾角调节近壁湍动能水平及其展向分布。

Abstract: The influence of rear-face inclination on turbulence around a surface-mounted protrusion depends on the separated flow approaching its trailing edge. This dependence is examined experimentally using stereoscopic particle image velocimetry over twelve trapezoidal protrusions with upper-surface length-to-height ratios $L/h=1,2,3,4$ and rear-face angles of $30^\circ$, $45^\circ$, and $90^\circ$. Measurements were conducted at a height-based Reynolds number of approximately $5\times10^4$, with an incoming boundary-layer thickness comparable to the protrusion height. For $L/h=1$ and 2, a connected reverse-flow region extends over the upper surface into the wake. For $L/h=3$ and 4, upper-surface reattachment separates the two regions. Length governs this change in topology and the variation in turbulence levels at common spatial locations, whereas rear-face inclination has its strongest influence close to the surface. From $L/h=2$ onward, shallower faces have higher near-face turbulent kinetic energy, while vertical faces have lower energy but a larger spanwise fraction. A decomposition of the in-plane deviatoric production separates the effects of Reynolds-stress and mean-strain magnitudes from those of their relative principal-axis orientation. At the inclined faces, this contribution changes from negative to positive between $L/h=1$ and 2, before upper-surface reattachment occurs. Its subsequent increase with length is driven mainly by increasing tensor magnitudes, with the largest contribution at $L/h=4$ and $30^\circ$, where strong production remains close to the rear face. These results show that local turbulence production depends jointly on the flow state approaching the trailing edge and the position of the energetic shear layer relative to the rear surface.

</details>


### [205] [Total Collision Energy model for surface processes in the DSMC method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01586)
*Yevgeniy A. Bondar,Alexandra N. Molchanova*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 将气相总碰撞能（TCE）方法拓展用于表面化学建模，提出sTCE模型，可由有限速率Arrhenius数据解析得到碰撞过程概率函数，并推广至给定反应效率表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直接模拟蒙特卡洛（DSMC）方法中，气相化学已通过总碰撞能（TCE）方法处理，但表面化学反应建模仍缺乏类似手段，且既有模型存在数学上的缺陷，因此需要将TCE思想移植到表面化学并加以改进。

Method: 将TCE方法应用于表面化学，提出表面TCE（sTCE）模型：依据有限速率Arrhenius数据解析推导碰撞过程概率关于分子平动能的函数；模型中可计入表面温度、入射角与内能的影响，并将模型推广到给定反应效率（specified reaction efficiency）的表述形式。

Result: 所导出的概率函数避免了以往模型常见的数学缺陷；sTCE模型能够处理表面温度、入射角及内能的影响，并可泛化为给定反应效率的形式。

Conclusion: sTCE模型为DSMC中的表面化学提供了一种数学上更稳健、物理因素覆盖更全面的建模途径。

Abstract: The total collision energy (TCE) approach originally developed for taking into account gas-phase chemistry in the direct simulation Monte Carlo method is applied to surface chemistry modeling. In the proposed surface TCE (sTCE) model probability of the impact process as a function of the translational energy of the molecule is found analytically from the finite-rate Arrhenius data. This probability function lacks some mathematical drawbacks typical of previous models. Effects of surface temperature, incident angle and internal energy can be taken into account in the sTCE model. Additionally the model is generalized to the specified reaction efficiency formulation.

</details>


### [206] [An unstructured finite-volume Helmholtz method with perfectly matched layers for heterogeneous two-phase acoustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01593)
*Chuanchao Xu,Jun Liu,Jan-Helge Dörsam,Mario Kupnik,Tomislav Maric,Dieter Bothe*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种用于时谐两相声学的单元中心非结构有限体积法，采用体积分数表示密度与可压缩性，结合复坐标拉伸PML和实虚分离块系统，并通过多类算例验证其精度与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非均质两相时谐声学问题，需要一种能在非结构网格上稳健处理界面、密度/可压缩性突变并支持PML截断的数值格式。

Method: 单元中心非结构有限体积法；体积分数表示密度与声学可压缩性；面分数基于相邻平面界面切割平均，保留单场面密度闭合；笛卡尔复坐标拉伸构建PML；实虚分离得到实值块系统，采用校正非正交通量与一致性边界处理进行离散。

Result: 均质波压力在正交及非正交内部/正交边界网格上约二阶收敛，重构速度在正交网格约二阶、另一类网格约1.7阶；分层气液透射在对齐加密下全域复压力相对L2误差降至2.657×10^-4；刚性球辐射力在测试Rayleigh尺寸范围内与Gorkov预测最大偏差1.5%。

Conclusion: 结果量化了该非均质Helmholtz-PML格式的精度与当前局限。

Abstract: This paper presents a cell-centred unstructured finite-volume method for time-harmonic two-phase acoustics. A volume-fraction representation supplies density and acoustic compressibility. Face fractions average available neighbouring planar interface cuts independently of velocity direction, retaining the established one-field face-density closure. Cartesian complex-coordinate stretching provides perfectly matched layers (PMLs). Real-imaginary splitting yields a real-valued block system, discretized with corrected non-orthogonal fluxes and consistent boundary contributions. Verification covers homogeneous waves, layered gas-liquid transmission with PML truncation, baffled-piston radiation with Kirchhoff far-field reconstruction, and resolved rigid-sphere radiation forces. Homogeneous-wave pressure converges at approximately second order on orthogonal meshes and on meshes with non-orthogonal interiors and orthogonal boundary cells. Reconstructed velocity converges at approximately second order on orthogonal meshes and with an observed order of about 1.7 on the latter mesh family. Under aligned refinement, the layered case's relative whole-domain complex-pressure $L_2$ error decreases to $2.657\times10^{-4}$. The resolved-sphere force differs from the Gorkov prediction by at most 1.5% over the tested Rayleigh size range. The results quantify the accuracy and current limitations of the heterogeneous Helmholtz-PML formulation.

</details>


### [207] [Femtosecond laser-induced cavitation seeds liquid-jet breakup beyond the Rayleigh-Plateau stability limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01597)
*Sourabh Singh,Tamanna,S. Sree Harsha,Prashant Kumar Singh*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 利用飞秒激光诱导空化在流动液柱上施加局域脉冲反冲扰动，独立调控扰动波数与幅值，实现跨越经典Rayleigh-Plateau稳定边界（kR=1）的射流破碎二维相图与可控液滴生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Rayleigh-Plateau理论预测液柱扰动仅在kR<1时增长，kR=1为线性稳定边界。本文旨在突破该边界限制，实现对射流破碎波数与幅值的独立、局域控制。

Method: 用飞秒激光在流动液柱内诱导空化，产生局域脉冲反冲扰动；通过激光重复频率设定扰动的波长（间距），通过脉冲能量设定扰动强度（幅值），在(kR, epsilon/R)平面上绘制射流破碎的二维图谱。

Result: 低幅值时，调节重复频率可重现经典RP色散关系及kR=1截断；增大波长可依次实现单分散液滴链、双分散液滴群及孤立单液滴。沿幅值轴，低脉冲能量仅放大预设波数，超过临界能量Ec后种子展宽并同时激发多个波数。更高幅值下实现kR>1（直至kR=1.9）的确定性破碎，液滴产生率与激光同步、最高达0.2 MHz。

Conclusion: 局域飞秒激光诱导空化可作为在经典线性稳定边界之内与之外实现可控Rayleigh-Plateau破碎的有效途径。

Abstract: The Rayleigh-Plateau instability predicts that infinitesimal perturbations of a liquid jet grow only for kR < 1, with kR = 1 marking the classical stability boundary. Here, we show that femtosecond-laser-induced cavitation generates a localized, impulsive recoil perturbation on a flowing liquid jet, enabling a two-dimensional map of jet breakup across the (kR, epsilon/R) plane. The perturbation's spacing (wavelength) and strength (amplitude) are set independently by the laser repetition rate and pulse energy, respectively. At low amplitudes, tuning the repetition rate recovers the classical Rayleigh-Plateau dispersion relation and its kR = 1 cutoff, while increasing the wavelength enables controlled generation of monodisperse droplet trains, bidisperse droplet populations, and ultimately isolated single droplets. Along the amplitude axis, low pulse energies produce a seed that amplifies only the imposed wavenumber, whereas above a critical energy Ec, the seed broadens to excite many wavenumbers simultaneously. At still higher amplitudes, we demonstrate deterministic breakup for kR > 1, extending to kR = 1.9, with laser-synchronized droplet production rates up to 0.2 MHz. These results establish localized femtosecond-laser-induced cavitation as a route to controlled Rayleigh-Plateau breakup both within and beyond the classical linear-stability boundary.

</details>


### [208] [On the transmission of floating-point perturbations in flow-dependent filter-width formulations in Large-Eddy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01743)
*Valerio D'Alessandro,Alessio Piccolo,Matteo Falone,Simone Bnà*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究异构高性能计算中浮点非结合性对LES最小二乘（LSQ）滤波宽度公式的扰动产生、传递与耦合机制，提出最大-最小压缩与有界调制的鲁棒化公式，并在CPU/GPU上通过圆柱绕流与Taylor-Green涡进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构HPC架构因浮点运算非结合性会给数值算法引入扰动；在LES中，这类数值效应在影响进入亚格子（SGS）模型的滤波宽度时变得重要，而LSQ滤波宽度公式对该扰动如何响应尚不清楚。

Method: 针对LSQ滤波宽度公式，分析浮点效应在固定解析运动学下相对于网格度量扰动的产生、传播与耦合；证明其对数非扩张但非严格收缩特性；提出方向性网格尺度的标量最大-最小压缩结合基于解析速度梯度的有界调制；在异构CPU与GPU上对Re=3900圆柱绕流和Re=1600 Taylor-Green涡进行评估。

Result: LSQ滤波宽度对网格度量扰动呈对数非扩张，扰动可经强方向性响应几乎无衰减传递；圆柱绕流中相对度量扰动的近一比一传递在扰动与剪切层转捩相互作用时变得相关；而正交Taylor-Green涡网格上积累路径结构上缺失，无宏观响应出现。

Conclusion: 所提公式通过滤波宽度算子重路由浮点扰动，在保留原LSQ流依赖特性的同时提升鲁棒性；结果表明在异构计算环境中浮点敏感性对LES滤波宽度公式具有实际意义。

Abstract: Heterogeneous high--performance computing architectures expose numerical algorithms to perturbations arising from the non--associativity of floating--point arithmetic. In Large--Eddy Simulation (LES), similar numerical effects may become relevant when they affect the filter--width entering the subgrid scale (SGS) model. This work investigates this mechanism for the least--squares (LSQ) based filter--width formulation, focusing on how floating--point effects are generated, transmitted, and coupled with the resolved flow. We show that, for fixed resolved kinematics, the LSQ filter--width is logarithmically non-expansive but not strictly contractive with respect to perturbations of the mesh metrics. Consequently, small disturbances may be transmitted with little attenuation through strongly directional filter-width responses. To mitigate this sensitivity, we introduce a scalar max--min compression of the directional mesh scales together with a bounded modulation based on the resolved velocity gradient.
  The resulting formulation reroutes floating--point perturbations through the filter-width operator, improving robustness while preserving the flow--dependent character of the original LSQ construction. The framework is assessed on heterogeneous CPU and GPU architectures for flow past a circular cylinder at Re=3900 and the Taylor--Green vortex at Re=1600. In the former, nearly one--to--one transmission of relative metric disturbances can become relevant when the transmitted perturbations interact with shear-layer transition. By contrast, on orthogonal Taylor--Green vortex grids, the accumulation pathway is structurally absent and no comparable macroscopic response develops. These results suggest floating--point sensitivity matters for LES filter--width formulations in heterogeneous computing environments.

</details>


### [209] [Direct Modeling of Pore Size Evolution and Microcollapse in Lyophilization by Population Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01851)
*Isaac Stonewall Wheeler,Vivek Narsimhan,Alina A. Alexeenko,Davide Fissore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对冷冻干燥一次干燥阶段中无定形辅料接近玻璃化转变温度时发生的"微观塌陷"现象，建立了基于群体平衡模型的孔径分布演化模型，并将其与传质阻力关联。模型通过矩量法和插值闭合保持与文献标准模型相当的计算成本。拟合结果表明，微观塌陷速率常数可能是与具体多孔几何无关的材料参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在制药冷冻干燥的一次干燥阶段，无定形辅料接近玻璃化转变温度时容易发生"微观塌陷"，导致传质阻力变化。现有建模方法要么完全回避这一区域，要么依赖繁琐的实验经验评估传质阻力的下降，缺乏机理性的预测手段。因此作者希望正面处理该现象，建立可预测且计算可行的机理模型。

Method: 建立描述冻干饼孔径分布演化的群体平衡模型（population balance model），将孔径与传质阻力相关联。采用矩量法（method of moments）和插值闭合（interpolative closure）处理群体平衡方程，使计算负担与一次干燥的文献标准模型相当。以文献实例数据对模型进行拟合与验证，包括高低产品温度条件下的拟合及中间条件的预测，以及另一组事后测量孔径分布的数据集验证传质阻力表达式。

Result: 对于传质阻力随产品温度明显变化的文献实例，模型在高低产品温度条件下拟合后，能够以同等精度模拟中间条件。在无微观塌陷配方的孔径分布实测数据集上，仅需拟合模型中的一个参数即可实现与事后经验评估相当的产品温度预测精度。对某一配方跨多条件的复杂多实验拟合实现了产品温度的定量一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的群体平衡模型能有效描述微观塌陷过程中的孔径演化及其对传质阻力的影响，且计算成本与文献标准模型相当。结果表明微观塌陷行为的速率常数可能是材料参数，与具体的多孔几何结构无关，为冷冻干燥过程建模提供了机理性的预测工具。

Abstract: In the primary drying step of pharmaceutical freeze drying, it is well known that amorphous excipients are prone to "microcollapse" as they approach their glass transition temperature. Existing modeling approaches for this phenomenon generally entail either avoidance of this regime or an experimentally burdensome empirical assessment of the resulting drop in resistance to mass transfer. In this work, we approach the phenomenon head-on by developing a population balance model for the pore size distribution of a freeze-dried cake during microcollapse and relating the pore size to mass transfer resistance. By treating the population balance with the method of moments and an interpolative closure, we keep the model's computational burden on par with the literature-standard model for primary drying.
  For a literature example when mass transfer resistance clearly varies as a function of product temperature during drying, we fit the population balance model to cases with high and low product temperature and show that it can then simulate intermediate cases with equal accuracy. The validity of the expression for mass transfer resistance is demonstrated with another literature data set, where pore size distributions were measured after the fact for a formulation without microcollapse; only a single parameter from this model needs to be fit to those cases to yield predictive accuracy in product temperature on par with post-hoc empirical assessment. Finally, a sophisticated multi-experiment fit for one formulation across a range of conditions yields quantitative agreement in product temperatures, suggesting that the rate constant for microcollapse behavior may be a material parameter and independent of the precise porous geometry.

</details>


### [210] [Rapid generation of relaxed centimeter-sized drops by capillary auto-ejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01857)
*Patric Mueller,Kai Ihrig,Achim Sack,Jonathan E. Kollmer,Thorsten Poeschel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究毛细自主喷射（"水坑跳跃"）作为被动产生自由悬浮液滴的机制，并优化腔体形状以缩短喷射后液滴的弛豫时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力实验常需厘米级球形液滴，而落塔实验仅提供数秒微重力时间，液滴须在此极短时间内生成并达到接近松弛的球状，因此需要优化被动生成机制以缩短弛豫时间。

Method: 针对填充固定液体积的抛物线截面腔体，通过最小化初始液表面积得到唯一形状；采用光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）模拟比较抛物线族内各形状的弛豫时间；并尝试在腔体中心引入浅凹陷以进一步缩短弛豫时间。

Result: 初始液表面积最小化对应的形状在抛物线族内也具有最短弛豫时间；引入浅中央凹陷虽使初始液表面积增大，却可进一步缩短弛豫时间。

Conclusion: 该研究为毛细驱动液滴发生器提供了设计准则，可在落塔有限的短微重力时段内快速产生松弛的厘米级液滴。

Abstract: Many microgravity experiments require centimeter-sized spherical liquid drops.
